Thank you, Madam Speaker. Of course, to the distinguished colleague of mine and to all ERC family, magandang hapon po sa atin. Madam Speaker, DOE sets energy policy.

**[00:17:59.100] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000053)

**[00:18:56.440] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000054-split-5)

**[00:18:59.060] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000055)

**[00:19:16.700] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000056)

**[00:19:29.060] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000057)

**[00:49:40.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000857)  
ERC determined that

**[00:49:42.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000858)  
the benchmark needed

**[00:49:44.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000859)  
to be updated

**[00:49:46.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000860)  
and reflect more decent

**[00:49:48.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000861)  
actual operating experience

**[00:49:50.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000862)  
and differences among

**[00:49:52.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000863)  
generation technologies.

**[00:49:54.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000864)  
The new rules establish technology

**[00:49:56.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000865)  
specific allowable equivalent

**[00:50:00.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000866)  
unplanned outage days

**[00:50:02.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000867)  
require adherence to planned

**[00:50:04.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000868)  
maintenance schedules

**[00:50:06.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000869)  
and post maintenance reports

**[00:50:08.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000870)  
and provide for compliance

**[00:50:10.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000871)  
plans and administrative

**[00:50:12.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000872)  
sanctions for persistent

**[00:50:14.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000873)  
non-compliance.

**[00:50:16.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000874)  
The old rules of interim

**[00:50:18.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000875)  
reliability rules

**[00:50:20.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000876)  
were established using a

**[00:50:22.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000877)  
five-year performance data

**[00:50:24.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000878)  
covering 2,000

**[00:50:26.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000879)  
15 to 2019.

**[00:50:28.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000880)  
So now, the revised rules

**[00:50:30.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000881)  
use 10 years

**[00:50:32.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000882)  
performance data from

**[00:50:34.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000883)  
2015 to

**[00:50:36.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000884)  
2024.

**[00:50:38.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000885)  
Madam Speaker.

**[00:50:40.720] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000886)  
Again, Madam Speaker, that will be my last

**[00:50:42.720] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000887)  
question. I fully

**[00:50:44.720] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000888)  
support

**[00:50:46.720] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000889)  
the budget of ERC.

**[00:50:48.720] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000890)  
Kung gusto natin na maging maayos

**[00:50:50.720] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000891)  
lahat, lalo ang

**[00:50:52.720] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000892)  
presyo ng ating kuryente, I think

**[00:50:54.720] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000893)  
we can start with the ERC

**[00:50:56.720] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000894)  
para mapabilis po, not only

**[00:50:58.720] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000895)  
the reset, the capex and other

**[00:51:00.720] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000896)  
works of

**[00:51:02.720] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000897)  
the ERC. Again, thank you very much

**[00:51:04.720] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000898)  
ERC family. Of course, kong Arne,

**[00:51:06.720] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000899)  
very well said,

**[00:51:08.720] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000900)  
kung Arne. Thank you very much.

**[00:51:10.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000901)  
Thank you,

**[00:51:12.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000902)  
Congressman [unclear].

Yes, thank you Madam Speaker.

**[00:51:26.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000909)

**[00:51:28.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000910)  
To my dear colleague,

**[00:51:30.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000911)  
Congressman Arne Pentevelia,

**[00:51:32.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000912)  
ang mga tanong ko po,

**[00:51:34.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000913)  
it will be mostly about the

**[00:51:36.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000914)

**[00:51:38.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000915)  
About the system loss.

**[00:51:40.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000916)  
Alam niyo po, yung system loss has been

**[00:51:42.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000917)  
my advocacy since

**[00:51:44.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000918)  
my first time in Congress in 2010.

**[00:51:48.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000919)  
Tatlong beses po ako nag-file ng bill tungkol sa

**[00:51:50.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000920)  
system loss, 2010, 2013,

**[00:51:52.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000921)

**[00:51:54.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000922)  
Kaya po nung SONA,

**[00:51:56.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000923)  
at sinabi po ng Pangulong

**[00:51:58.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000924)  
Marcos, natatanggalin na po yung

**[00:52:00.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000925)  
system loss. Ay tuwang-tuwa po ako.

**[00:52:02.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000926)  
Ngayon, my first

**[00:52:04.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000927)  
question addressed to the ERC

**[00:52:06.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000928)  
is

**[00:52:08.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000929)  
yung VAT on system loss,

**[00:52:10.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000930)

**[00:52:12.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000931)

**[00:52:14.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000932)  
ito ng ating mga consumers?

**[00:52:16.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000933)  
Madam Speaker.

**[00:52:20.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000934)  
Madam Speaker,

**[00:52:22.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000935)  
the resolution was already out

**[00:52:24.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000936)  
this September.

**[00:52:26.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000937)  
The implementation

**[00:52:28.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000938)  
will be

**[00:52:30.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000939)  
by

**[00:52:32.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000940)  
the October billing.

**[00:52:34.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000941)  
Siguro po by October,

**[00:52:36.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000942)  
mararamdaman na po yan.

**[00:52:38.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000943)  
Lalo na talaga pong

**[00:52:40.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000944)  
dapat po hindi i-VAT

**[00:52:42.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000945)  
ang system loss.

**[00:52:44.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000946)  
Maraming salamat po, dear colleague.

**[00:52:46.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000947)  
Pangalawa po. So I may add, Madam Speaker,

**[00:52:48.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000948)  
hindi lang po resolution ng ERC

**[00:52:50.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000949)  
and even BIR.

**[00:52:52.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000950)  
Maraming salamat po, dear colleague.

**[00:52:54.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000951)  
Pangalawa po, diba po

**[00:52:56.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000952)  
ang system loss ay sinasabi po nila

**[00:52:58.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000953)  
ay dalawa po.

**[00:53:00.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000954)  
Yung technical at yung non-technical.

**[00:53:02.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000955)  
Yes.

**[00:53:04.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000956)  
Punahin po muna natin yung

**[00:53:06.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000957)  
yung non-technical, no?

**[00:53:08.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000958)  
Yung pilferage and all these things.

**[00:53:10.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000959)  
Possibly po ba

**[00:53:12.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000960)  
na

**[00:53:16.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000961)  
Possibly po ba

**[00:53:18.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000962)  
na gumawa na lang

**[00:53:20.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000963)  
ang ERC na resolution

**[00:53:22.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000964)  
para tanggalin na yung non-technical

**[00:53:24.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000965)  
system loss?

**[00:53:26.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000966)  
O talagang iintayin pa yung batas

**[00:53:28.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000967)  
na ipapasa ng Kongreso?

**[00:53:30.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000968)  
Yes, thank you for that, Mr. Speaker.

**[00:53:32.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000969)  
Actually, that is the resolution already

**[00:53:34.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000970)  
na ginawa po ng

**[00:53:36.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000971)  
ERC.

**[00:53:38.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000972)  
It's for the non-technical.

**[00:53:42.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000973)  
But,

**[00:53:44.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000974)  
yun sa non-technical, we are studying

**[00:53:46.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000975)  
lowering the cap kasi po

**[00:53:48.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000976)  
may threshold po yan.

**[00:53:50.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000977)  
Non-technical po rin natin, no?

**[00:53:52.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000978)  
So, regarding

**[00:53:54.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000979)  
sa VAT, dapat tanggalin.

**[00:53:56.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000980)  
But, we are studying yung

**[00:53:58.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000981)  
threshold. How

**[00:54:00.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-000982)  
we can lower the cap.

**[00:54:02.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000983)  
Madam Speaker, dear

**[00:54:04.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000984)  
colleague, ang intindi ko po kasi

**[00:54:06.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000985)  
doon sa mensahe ng Pangulo

**[00:54:08.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000986)  
is tanggalin.

**[00:54:10.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000987)  
Tanggalin means zero

**[00:54:12.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000988)  
technical loss, no? For both

**[00:54:14.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000989)  
technical and non-technicals.

**[00:54:16.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000990)  
Pero, since yung

**[00:54:18.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000991)  
non-technical,

**[00:54:20.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000992)  
kaya namang pigilan.

**[00:54:22.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000993)  
Diba? Kaya naman ma-prevent.

**[00:54:24.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000994)  
Posible ba

**[00:54:26.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000995)  
na gumawa na lang yung ERC

**[00:54:28.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000996)  
ng resolution

**[00:54:30.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000997)  
para hindi na makolekta

**[00:54:32.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000998)  
yung non-technical system loss?

**[00:54:34.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-000999)  
O kailangan po ba talaga

**[00:54:36.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001000)  
na indayin muna

**[00:54:38.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001001)  
yung batas na ipapasa ng Kongreso

**[00:54:40.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001002)  
bago po

**[00:54:42.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001003)  
mapigilan yung

**[00:54:44.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001004)  
pagsingil ng non-technical

**[00:54:46.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001005)  
system loss?

**[00:54:47.700] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001005-split-2)  
No need for

**[00:54:48.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001006)  
law, Madam Speaker.

**[00:54:50.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001007)  
Yes, you can do it.

**[00:54:52.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001008)  
So, that is very good news. So, kailan po

**[00:54:54.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001009)  
maipapatupad ng ERC

**[00:54:56.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001010)  
na tanggalin yung

**[00:54:58.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001011)  
non-technical system loss?

**[00:55:00.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001012)  
Yes, hindi lang po kasi

**[00:55:02.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001013)  
ganung kadali, just to implement.

**[00:55:04.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001014)  
We have to be

**[00:55:06.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001015)  
in touch also with

**[00:55:08.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001016)  
the policy of

**[00:55:10.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001017)  
DOE. So,

**[00:55:12.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001018)  
actually yung ginagawa po natin na

**[00:55:14.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001019)  
by October doon lang po sa billing

**[00:55:16.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001020)  
na lalo na tanggalin na

**[00:55:18.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001021)  
yung bag-EVAT, yung

**[00:55:20.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001022)  
system loss, ay napakabilis

**[00:55:22.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001023)  
na po nung ginagawa ng ERC.

**[00:55:24.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001024)  
But it's very complex.

**[00:55:26.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001025)  
It's not so easy, especially

**[00:55:28.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001026)  
when we talk about the technical aspect.

**[00:55:30.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001027)  
Dito lang po tayo

**[00:55:32.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001028)  
nag-uusap sa non-technical.

**[00:55:34.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001029)  
But pinag-uusapan rin po ng

**[00:55:36.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001030)  
DOE and ERC

**[00:55:38.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001031)  
kung paano babantayan po ang BIR.

**[00:55:40.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001032)  
Because iba-iba pong

**[00:55:42.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001033)  
electric cooperative, iba-iba rin po

**[00:55:44.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001034)  
yung threshold. Iba po yung

**[00:55:46.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001035)  
sa Meralco, iba po yung sa

**[00:55:48.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001036)  
Casureco IV, iba po

**[00:55:50.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001037)  
yung sa Mindanao. So,

**[00:55:52.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001038)  
there should be a

**[00:55:54.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001039)  
composite team between

**[00:55:56.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001040)  
the DOE policy making,

**[00:55:58.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001041)  
ERC, and even

**[00:56:00.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001042)  
the BIR. So, medyo

**[00:56:02.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001043)  
yun po ang medyo pinupulido

**[00:56:04.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001044)  
pa natin. But definitely,

**[00:56:06.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001045)  
bababa po by computation

**[00:56:08.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001046)  
is about 20 pesos

**[00:56:12.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001047)  
per, I think, per month.

**[00:56:14.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001048)  
That's for

**[00:56:16.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001049)  
the system loss. Dahil po,

**[00:56:18.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001050)  
tatanggalin po sa VAT.

**[00:56:20.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001051)  
Madam Speaker.

**[00:56:22.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001052)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[00:56:24.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001053)  
Tama po, naiintindihan ko po yun sa VAT.

**[00:56:26.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001054)  
Ang tanong ko lang po ngayon is concentrated

**[00:56:28.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001055)  
on the system loss itself.

**[00:56:30.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001056)  
So, kung pwede po yung

**[00:56:32.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001057)  
ERC na gumawa ng resolution

**[00:56:34.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001058)  
at tatanggalin

**[00:56:36.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001059)  
na yung

**[00:56:38.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001060)  
non-technical,

**[00:56:40.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001061)  
purely non-technical,

**[00:56:42.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001062)  
Madam Speaker, purely non-technical

**[00:56:44.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001063)  
system loss ay tatanggalin

**[00:56:46.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001064)  
na, kailan po ito

**[00:56:48.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001065)  
na i-implement ng ERC?

**[00:56:50.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001066)  
Are we talking

**[00:56:52.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001067)  
about, Madam Speaker, just for clarification

**[00:56:54.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001068)  
lang, are we talking about the CAP or

**[00:56:56.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001069)  
the VAT?

**[00:56:58.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001070)  
Hindi. Ang pinag-uusapan natin dito is

**[00:57:00.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001071)  
yung non-technical system loss

**[00:57:02.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001072)  
na gagawing zero.

**[00:57:04.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001073)  
Gagawing zero. Kasi ang sabi po ng

**[00:57:06.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001074)  
Presidente is, wala na.

**[00:57:08.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001075)  
So, kung wala na, ang ibig sabihin po

**[00:57:10.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001076)  
nun ay zero.

**[00:57:12.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001077)  
So, yun po ang tanong ko, Madam Speaker.

**[00:57:14.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001078)  
Yes, thank you for that. We're still

**[00:57:16.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001079)  
studying

**[00:57:18.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001080)  
Madam Speaker, in

**[00:57:20.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001081)  
coordination with DOE,

**[00:57:22.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001082)  
kung zero CAP

**[00:57:24.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001083)  
ang ipapataw po natin for

**[00:57:26.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001084)  
the non-technical,

**[00:57:28.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001085)  
at saka kung kailan po. So,

**[00:57:30.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001086)  
it's still a work in progress,

**[00:57:32.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001087)  
Madam Speaker.

**[00:57:34.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001088)  
Yes, Madam Speaker. Pero kahit po

**[00:57:36.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001089)  
work in progress, sana naman may timetable.

**[00:57:38.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001090)  
Diba? Dahil tayo pong

**[00:57:40.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001091)  
tapong gobyerno, dapat po

**[00:57:42.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001092)  
pag sinabi natin doon sa ating

**[00:57:44.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001093)  
mga kababayan, alam po nila

**[00:57:46.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001094)  
hanggang kailan po sila maging intay.

**[00:57:48.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001095)  
Actually, regarding, I truly understand

**[00:57:50.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001096)  
and I truly agree with

**[00:57:52.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001097)  
Congressman Tobichanko, na kailangan po talaga

**[00:57:54.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001098)  
may timetable, hindi lang po puro pangako

**[00:57:56.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001099)  
po ito. But nakita naman po natin

**[00:57:58.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001100)  
yung aksyon kaagad ng ERC

**[00:58:00.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001101)  
and even ng BIR. But iba po

**[00:58:02.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001102)  
kasi yung VAT

**[00:58:04.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001103)  
na pinag-uusapan, iba rin po yung CAP.

**[00:58:06.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001104)  
So, actually,

**[00:58:08.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001105)  
sa buong mundo po, sa buong

**[00:58:10.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001106)  
nasyon, buong mundo,

**[00:58:12.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001107)  
I think,

**[00:58:14.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001108)  
I think, first time po ito

**[00:58:16.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001109)  
na Pilipinas

**[00:58:18.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001110)  
lang ang

**[00:58:20.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001111)  
hindi magbabayad ng system loss. And just to

**[00:58:22.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001112)  
clarify, Madam Speaker,

**[00:58:24.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001113)  
kasi hindi po ganong naintindihan

**[00:58:26.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001114)  
ng ating mga kababayan.

**[00:58:28.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001115)  
Ang system loss po ay

**[00:58:30.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001116)  
parang halimbawa,

**[00:58:32.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001117)  
bumili po tayo ng tela

**[00:58:34.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001118)  
ng barong. Sinukat

**[00:58:36.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001119)  
po sa inyo.

**[00:58:38.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001120)  
Siyempre po, may natanggal.

**[00:58:40.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001121)  
Pero binayaran mo yung buo.

**[00:58:42.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001122)  
So, ganito po rin,

**[00:58:44.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001123)  
sa system loss. But,

**[00:58:46.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001124)  
we fully understand the direction

**[00:58:48.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001125)  
of the President. That's why

**[00:58:50.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001126)  
ERC

**[00:58:52.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001127)  
and even DOE are

**[00:58:54.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001128)  
working on it. They're

**[00:58:56.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001129)  
working hard.

**[00:58:58.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001130)  
Medyo tayo lang po ito.

**[00:59:00.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001131)  
Kinukonsiderin natin yung

**[00:59:02.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001132)  
financial viability

**[00:59:04.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001133)  
ng mga DUs. Because we have

**[00:59:06.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001134)  
private and we have electric

**[00:59:08.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001135)  
cooperatives. And alam na po natin ang

**[00:59:10.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001136)  
electric cooperatives ay

**[00:59:12.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001137)  
non-profit. So,

**[00:59:14.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001138)  
kung mag-upgrade po sila

**[00:59:16.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001139)  
ng kanyang niya, poste,

**[00:59:18.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001140)  
transformer, saan po sila

**[00:59:20.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001141)  
kukuha ng kanila pong

**[00:59:22.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001142)  
kapital? So, ito po yung mga

**[00:59:24.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001143)  
kinukonsider po natin bago po

**[00:59:26.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001144)  
natin fully ma-implement

**[00:59:28.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001145)  
ang inuutos po ng ating

**[00:59:30.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001146)  
pag-removal ng cap ng ating system

**[00:59:32.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001147)  
sauce sa non-technical pasa.

**[00:59:34.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001148)  
Yes po. Madam Speaker,

**[00:59:36.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001149)  
dear colleague, pasensya na po.

**[00:59:38.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001150)  
Dear colleague, I cannot agree on the

**[00:59:40.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001151)  
analogy doon sa tela.

**[00:59:42.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001152)  
Kasi po ang tela, pag bumili

**[00:59:44.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001153)  
tayo ng 2.5 meters na tela

**[00:59:46.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001154)  
at ginawa natin barong, hindi

**[00:59:48.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001155)  
nasa talaga 2.5 meters. Agree po ako

**[00:59:50.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001156)  
doon. Pero ang magbabayad

**[00:59:52.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001157)  
doon sa nawalang tela is mismo

**[00:59:54.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001158)  
sino yung nagpagawa ng barong.

**[00:59:56.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001159)  
Pero yung system loss po kasi,

**[00:59:58.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001160)  
iba po ito.

**[01:00:00.000] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001161)  
Ang nangyayari, as sa tingin ko, the proper analogy of the system loss is,

**[01:00:04.320] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001162)  
tayong lahat dito, kumain tayo sa restaurant.

**[01:00:07.760] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001163)  
Merong hindi nagbayad, pumuslit, so nanakawan.

**[01:00:12.840] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001164)  
Ang system loss, ang nangyayari, kung sino yung natira dito,

**[01:00:17.300] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001165)  
sila yung maghahati-hating bayaran yung kinain nung hindi nagbayad sa restaurant.

**[01:00:22.580] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001166)  
Kasi ganun po yun, yung system loss, sinisingil doon sa mga nagbabayad,

**[01:00:26.780] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001167)  
hindi po sinisingil doon sa hindi nagbayad.

**[01:00:28.700] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001168)  
Sa tumatakas.

**[01:00:29.400] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001169)  
Yes, totally agree.

**[01:00:30.780] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001170)  
Okay, anyway, I will concentrate on the, all my questions will be about the non-technical system loss.

**[01:00:39.080] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001171)  
Yung technical system loss, hindi naman po ako magaling sa technical, so hindi po ako magtatanong.

**[01:00:44.320] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001172)  
Pero yung non-technical system loss, pinakamalaki dyan ay tungkol doon sa pilferage, tama po ba?

**[01:00:50.580] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001173)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[01:00:54.080] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001174)  
So kung pilferage yan, hindi...

**[01:00:57.920] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001175)  
Ang isang malaking dahilan ng pilferage,

**[01:00:59.400] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001176)  
is minsan kasi, for example, ang experience ko sa Meralco,

**[01:01:03.880] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001177)  
I cannot speak about electrical cooperatives, no?

**[01:01:07.980] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001178)  
Napipilitan, hindi ko naman tinotolerate,

**[01:01:11.220] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001179)  
pero pag ang isang bahay, hindi nila kinabitan ng kuryente,

**[01:01:16.380] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001180)  
dahil yung bahay na yun ay informal settler,

**[01:01:21.160] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001181)  
hindi naman mabubuhay yung tao na walang kuryente.

**[01:01:24.920] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001182)  
Okay.

**[01:01:25.920] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001183)  
So ang gagawin ng taong yun is,

**[01:01:29.400] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001184)  
malamang magja-jumper.

**[01:01:32.060] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001185)  
At yun ang magiging pilferage, at yun ang magiging system loss na sinisingil sa atin.

**[01:01:38.120] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001186)  
Kasi dalawa lang yun, either bumili sa ilan ng submitter,

**[01:01:41.600] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001187)  
siyempre mas mahal yun, di ba?

**[01:01:42.840] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001188)  
Papatong yung may mother meter,

**[01:01:44.980] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001189)  
o magja-jumper sila.

**[01:01:47.200] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001190)  
So hindi lang system loss ang problema dito, it's also a fire hazard, Madam Speaker.

**[01:01:53.220] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001191)  
Ngayon, ang requirement kasi ng Meralco is,

**[01:01:57.100] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001192)  
dapat mag-present ng titulo, o dapat may consent ng owner.

**[01:02:04.820] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001193)  
Siyempre, yung may-ari na privado,

**[01:02:09.400] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001194)  
hindi rin po siya papayag magbigay ng consent,

**[01:02:12.800] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001195)  
kasi kadalasan, gusto nilang paalisin yung informal settler.

**[01:02:18.080] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001196)  
So para mapilitan pumalis yung informal settler,

**[01:02:21.260] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001197)  
hindi nila bibigyan ng right of way, di ba?

**[01:02:25.900] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001198)  
O ng authorization.

**[01:02:27.100] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001199)  
Sa gobyerno, mas simple, kasi kami, sa Navotas, practical po kami.

**[01:02:34.960] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001200)  
Pag government land po yan, binibigyan namin ng authority na makapagkabit.

**[01:02:40.600] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001201)  
Dahil alam namin na hindi sila mabubuhay ng walang kuryente,

**[01:02:43.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001202)  
at magiging fire hazard kung magja-jumper sila.

**[01:02:47.920] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001203)  
So ngayon, ang proposal ko noon, noon pong budget briefing,

**[01:02:53.060] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001204)  
sabi ko, dapat baguhin ng ERC yung requirement.

**[01:02:57.100] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001205)  
Huwag na nilang hingan ng right of way o authority nung private land owner po,

**[01:03:04.840] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001206)  
kasi hindi naman magbibigay yung private land owner.

**[01:03:08.000] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001207)  
But yung pagbigay naman ng kuryente, kasi madam speaker, dear colleague,

**[01:03:13.900] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001208)  
sana po maintindihan nyo, di ba?

**[01:03:15.160] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001209)  
Pag may informal settler, madalas nag-aaway yung informal settler,

**[01:03:18.820] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001210)  
tsaka yung may-ari ng lupa.

**[01:03:20.260] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001211)  
So natural, hindi magbibigay ng authority o ng consent yung may-ari ng lupa.

**[01:03:27.100] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001212)  
So ang sabi ko noon, hindi ba dapat ang gawin ng ERC dyan is huwag na i-require yung titulo,

**[01:03:40.100] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001213)  
huwag na i-require yung consent para lahat makabitan.

**[01:03:45.620] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001214)  
Pag lahat kinakabitan, wala na silang dahilan kung bakit sila nagja-jumper.

**[01:03:49.840] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001215)  
Yun po ang tanong ko po, madam speaker, dear colleague.

**[01:03:52.940] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001216)  
Yes, madam speaker, that's a very good observation.

**[01:03:56.300] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001217)  
And I'm happy.

**[01:03:57.100] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001218)  
I'm happy to tell our good colleague that in amend na po yung ERC,

**[01:04:03.420] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001219)  
ang magna-karta, September 10, pinadali na po ito.

**[01:04:07.080] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001220)  
So hindi na po kailangan ng titulo.

**[01:04:09.020] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001221)  
Ang kailangan nila po, kahit certification po ng barangay captain,

**[01:04:12.140] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001222)  
na ikaw po talagang taga dyan, residente dyan.

**[01:04:15.660] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001223)  
So yun na po yung pinapasunod ng anti-ERC sa mga de-use natin po.

**[01:04:21.380] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001224)  
So September 10, 2026 po naging effective.

**[01:04:23.820] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001225)  
Ito po, September 10 pa lang po.

**[01:04:25.500] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001226)  
So salamat po.

**[01:04:27.100] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001227)  
Anong sa committee ay nagawa na po ng formal na aksyon?

**[01:04:30.780] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001228)  
Yes.

**[01:04:31.120] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001229)  
Okay.

**[01:04:31.860] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001230)  
Ang sunod ko pong tanong is,

**[01:04:34.620] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001231)  
pag ang isang bahay, nahulihan na nag-jumper,

**[01:04:39.000] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001232)  
dinidemanda po yan ng Meralco, tama?

**[01:04:41.640] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001233)  
Yes, they should.

**[01:04:42.600] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001234)  
Okay.

**[01:04:43.740] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001235)  
Ang gagamitin ng Meralco na computation doon sa sisingilin nila

**[01:04:50.200] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001236)  
is titignan lahat ng appliances,

**[01:04:53.280] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001237)  
tapos ikukumpute yun, yun ang sisingilin doon sa nag-jumper.

**[01:04:57.100] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001238)  
Pero, Madam Speaker, Dear Colleague,

**[01:05:03.040] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001239)  
itong sinisingil nila doon sa nag-jumper ay nasingil na rin nila as system loss.

**[01:05:10.800] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001240)  
So hindi ba doble singil yun?

**[01:05:12.660] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001241)  
Pag nakolektahan nila yung nag-jumper,

**[01:05:19.760] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001242)  
papaanong ino-audit ng ERC na i-bawas yun naman doon sa siningil as system loss?

**[01:05:25.900] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001243)  
Ay, liliwanagin.

**[01:05:27.100] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001244)  
I-bawas yun po ulit, di ba, Madam Speaker?

**[01:05:29.080] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001245)  
Are you talking about the pilferage recovery loss?

**[01:05:31.740] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001246)  
Hindi, di ba po?

**[01:05:33.880] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001247)  
Yung lahat ng total ng pilferage, yun po ang non-technical system loss.

**[01:05:41.660] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001248)  
So, total yan, yun po ang i-charge sa mga consumers na system loss.

**[01:05:47.660] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001249)  
Pero may cap, di ba?

**[01:05:49.040] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001250)  
Yes.

**[01:05:49.280] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001251)  
May cap.

**[01:05:50.540] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001252)  
So, portion of that, na-recover din nung utility.

**[01:05:55.080] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001253)  
Tama po.

**[01:05:57.100] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001254)  
Yes.

**[01:05:58.840] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001255)  
Portion na-recover niya as system loss.

**[01:06:02.100] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001256)  
Ngayon, dinimanda po niya yung, at nahulihan nila ng jumper.

**[01:06:10.960] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001257)  
Kung nagbayad po yung dinimanda nila, naka-recover din sila ng pera.

**[01:06:16.760] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001258)  
So, papaanong ina-account at minomonitor ng ERC na yung nakolekta ng system loss ay binawas?

**[01:06:27.100] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001260)  
Doon.

**[01:06:27.680] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001261)  
Kasi otherwise, magiging doble bayad yan.

**[01:06:31.280] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001262)  
Yes.

**[01:06:32.000] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001263)  
So, dapat doon sa susunod na system loss, make a credit.

**[01:06:35.500] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001264)  
For example, for example lang, para lang magbigay tayo ng figure.

**[01:06:38.860] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001265)  
For example, may nawalang kuryente.

**[01:06:41.440] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001266)  
Nakasigil yung ERC ng, nakasigil yung electric utility ng limang piso.

**[01:06:47.320] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001267)  
Limang piso.

**[01:06:48.920] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001268)  
Dahil 5% ang cap, di ba?

**[01:06:50.860] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001269)  
For example, limang piso nakolekta dahil 5% yung cap.

**[01:06:54.180] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001270)  
Later on,

**[01:06:55.980] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001271)  
kinumpute nila,

**[01:06:57.100] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001272)  
later on po, kinumpute nila yung sisingilin doon sa nag-jumper.

**[01:07:04.340] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001273)  
At ang nakumpute nilang sisingilin ay 100 piso.

**[01:07:08.820] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001274)  
Pag hindi nila binawas yung limang pisong unang nakolekta,

**[01:07:13.300] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001275)  
ibig sabihin, 105 ang total na nakolekta nila instead of 100 pesos.

**[01:07:19.660] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001276)  
So, yung system loss na nakolekta,

**[01:07:22.180] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001277)  
papaano namang ibabawas, paano sinisigurado ng ERC

**[01:07:25.960] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001278)  
na ibabawas naman doon sa system loss na kukolektahin sa susunod

**[01:07:30.500] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001279)  
noong electrical utility?

**[01:07:32.720] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001280)  
Yun po.

**[01:07:33.380] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001281)  
Yes, may formula po yan, Madam Speaker,

**[01:07:36.440] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001282)  
regarding the rebate.

**[01:07:38.640] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001283)  
Pag parang nag-overcharge,

**[01:07:40.980] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001284)

**[01:07:43.380] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001285)  
Ang ginagamit po ng ERC,

**[01:07:45.260] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001286)  
sa pagkakulit yung generation charge,

**[01:07:49.880] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001287)

**[01:07:52.100] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001288)

**[01:07:53.600] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001289)

**[01:07:55.960] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001290)

**[01:07:58.980] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001291)  
Okay.

**[01:08:00.360] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001292)  
Panguling tanong na lang po, Madam Speaker, dear colleague.

**[01:08:06.180] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001293)

**[01:08:09.420] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001294)

**[01:08:14.680] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001295)

**[01:08:22.980] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001297)

**[01:08:25.960] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001298)

**[01:08:27.840] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001299)

**[01:08:55.960] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-001313)

**[01:15:00.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001498)  
Maraming salamat Madam Speaker.

**[01:15:03.320] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001499)  
Ilang dagdag na katanungan na lang na paglilinaw dahil po napakayaman na ng naging talakayan ng ating mga naunang interpellators.

**[01:15:13.140] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001500)  
Ngayon naman po, nabanggit kanina ng ating sponsor ang tungkol sa pending application ng Meralco para sa rate reset,

**[01:15:21.320] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001501)  
kung saan humihingi sila ng approval para sa revenue requirement na humigit kumulang P532.13 billion na sisingilin sa mga consumer mula taong 2027 hanggang 2030.

**[01:15:35.360] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001502)  
Para sa 2027, ang proposed maximum average price ng Meralco ay nasa P2.3436 per kilowatt hour mula sa P1.35 per kilowatt hour.

**[01:15:46.800] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001503)  
Nabanggit din po kanina ang update dito ay,

**[01:15:49.820] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001504)  
bago magtapos ang taong 2026 ay magkakaroon na ng formal na desisyon dito ang ERC.

**[01:15:57.900] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001505)  
Ngunit Madam Speaker, noong February 2026, sa mga pahayag ng ERC,

**[01:16:06.140] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001506)  
balak po nila o yung target sana nila na matapos ito, magkaroon ng formal na desisyon, ay nasa August to September this year.

**[01:16:14.680] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001507)  
Maari po bang malaman kung ano yung mga dahilan?

**[01:16:18.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001508)  
Kung bakit po?

**[01:16:20.560] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001509)  
Ngayon, sinasabi na natin na hindi na kaya ng August-September ang formal desisyon at mga nga ilangan pa ng mas mahabang oras ang ERC,

**[01:16:28.940] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001510)  
bagamat ang target ninyo ay matapos na ito bago ang taong 2026.

**[01:16:37.060] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001511)  
Dahil po malaki yung application, marami pong CAPEX application,

**[01:16:42.840] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001512)  
at saka yung mga assets yung po ng Meralco na nirefile,

**[01:16:47.100] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001513)  
so we need to verify, we have to be very prudent also, we have to follow the process.

**[01:16:57.340] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001514)  
But the process usually 18 months, but now sinorten natin na into 8 months.

**[01:17:03.300] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001515)  
So ganun po yung mga reforma na ginawa po ng ERC.

**[01:17:08.520] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001516)  
Kaya kung makikita niya po yung mga desisyon ng ERC, simula po na umupo ang ating pong chairman,

**[01:17:15.320] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001517)  
comparing to the...

**[01:17:17.100] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001518)  
In the past, marami na pong nagawa, pero kulang, kulang pa.

**[01:17:22.960] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001519)  
Kailangan ng tulong, kailangan po ng tulong.

**[01:17:25.320] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001520)  
Kailangan ng additional budget, sobrang kailangan po ng additional budget.

**[01:17:30.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001521)  
Madam Speaker, at tungkol naman din sa isa, sa mga sinisingil at pinapasa ng mga consumer,

**[01:17:38.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001522)  
ay tungkol sa naaprubahan nitong August 2026 na provisional increase ng feed and tariff allowance.

**[01:17:47.100] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001523)  
O ng fit all, mula 0.2011 peso tungo sa 0.3359 per kilowatt hour, o 67% na increase.

**[01:17:58.260] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001524)  
Dahil ayon sa kanila, sa liquidity problem ng fit all fund, para sa isang household na gumagamit ng 200 kilowatt hour kada buwan,

**[01:18:09.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001525)  
ito po ay nasa humigit kumulang, 67.18 pesos sa kanilang mga bela.

**[01:18:15.480] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001526)  
Ang original application...

**[01:18:17.100] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001527)  
Ang original application ng Transco ay nasa 0.2154 lamang per kilowatt hour,

**[01:18:22.460] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001528)  
pero ang provisional rate na naaprubahan ng ERC ay nasa 0.3359 per kilowatt hour.

**[01:18:30.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001529)  
Ito po ay mas mataas, mas malaki, kaysa dun sa hiningi mismo ng Transco.

**[01:18:36.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001530)  
Ano ba ang naging batayan para maging 0.3359 ang provisional rate na naaprubahan ng ERC?

**[01:18:42.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001531)  
Madam Speaker,

**[01:18:42.960] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001532)  
From the original, Madam Speaker, may hiningi silang additional, kasi may shortfall na nga po na nakita sa fit all fund.

**[01:18:57.620] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001533)  
And this also to help, lalo ng ating pong mga REs.

**[01:19:01.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001534)  
But ito po yung talagang reason, fit all collection was suspended December 2022 until January 2024.

**[01:19:11.840] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001535)  
Delays in the...

**[01:19:12.960] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001536)  
Approval of fit all to update the rates when it resumed collection.

**[01:19:18.320] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001537)  
2024, fit all was only approved March 2024.

**[01:19:24.740] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001538)  
And 2025, fit all was only approved November 2025.

**[01:19:30.960] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001539)  
As of August, 356 million of payable outstanding.

**[01:19:37.020] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001540)  
By December, this will be fully paid, Madam Speaker.

**[01:19:42.320] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001541)  
Madam Speaker,

**[01:19:42.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001542)  
Madam Speaker, Mr. Sponsor, hingi na lang po natin yung detalye ng mga bilang, lalo-lalo na.

**[01:19:48.480] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001543)  
Marami po talagang delay yung mga action dati, so ngayon po talaga nag-ahabol po tayo.

**[01:19:54.000] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001544)  
Pero maganda lang pong ginawa, instead of yung backlog yung ayusin, ayusin mo po na natin yung now.

**[01:20:01.120] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001545)  
Pag natapos po yung ngayon, balikan po natin yung mga backlogs po natin.

**[01:20:05.300] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001546)  
Ang buong fit all collections, ang ulat dito ay hinihingi ng representasyon na ito mula po sa ERC.

**[01:20:11.760] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001547)  
Yes, we will...

**[01:20:12.800] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001548)  
Submit, Madam Speaker.

**[01:20:14.840] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001549)  
At ganoon din po, nababahala tayo dahil ang bumapasan nito ay mga consumer.

**[01:20:19.460] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001550)  
Kaya kumbaga, sa ngayon ba pinag-aaralan po ng ERC, yung posible na pagtatanggal nito?

**[01:20:27.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001551)  
At pagtatanggal nito sa pasanin ng mga consumer at paghahanap ng iba pong source ng funding?

**[01:20:34.840] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001552)  
Yes, truly agree.

**[01:20:36.360] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001553)  
That's why we do the reset.

**[01:20:38.680] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001554)  
We really need to do the reset.

**[01:20:40.240] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001555)  
It's a means of...

**[01:20:42.160] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001556)  
accountability and even transparency.

**[01:20:45.460] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001557)  
Lalo na sa pag-apply po ng rates na po nagsimulayan sa CAPEX.

**[01:20:49.260] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001558)  
So, yan nga po, inabutan lang po tayo ng circumstance ng ganito.

**[01:20:54.060] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001559)  
But, hindi po magandang rason na dahil po marami pong backlog noon.

**[01:20:58.780] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001560)  
But you can see, ERC is working on it already.

**[01:21:02.600] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001561)  
Sa katunayan, Madam Speaker, nitong July 2026,

**[01:21:07.080] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001562)  
naghahain ang representasyon na ito ng isang panukalang resolusyon.

**[01:21:11.120] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001563)  
Ito ang pilang 1189.

**[01:21:14.940] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001564)  
At ang panawagan nga po natin ay maimbestigahan, makita, kung ano ba yung status ng collection,

**[01:21:23.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001565)  
kumusta ba yung pinagkakagastusan, at ano-ano ba yung iba't-ibang mga fees na sinisingil,

**[01:21:31.340] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001566)  
na pinapasan ng ating mga consumer dahil sa mga ulat, halimbawa po ng overbilling,

**[01:21:38.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001567)

**[01:21:41.120] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001568)  
ganun din po ang sobrang-sobrang taas na na singilin sa kuryente.

**[01:21:47.560] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001569)  
Hindi pa po ito nasisimulan na matalakay dito sa Kongreso at hanggang natin na matalakay na agad

**[01:21:54.100] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001570)  
para mabusisi natin yung haba ng listahan ng mga pinapasan at binabalikat ng ating mga consumer.

**[01:22:01.340] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001571)  
Saan ba talaga napupunta?

**[01:22:03.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001572)  
At kung paano pa natin ito mababawasan, paano natin matitiyak ang murang kuryente,

**[01:22:08.540] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001573)  
ang tunay na murang kuryente.

**[01:22:10.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001574)  
Ngayon ho,

**[01:22:11.120] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001575)  
tungkol doon sa mga nabanggit ko ng mga reports,

**[01:22:16.100] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001576)  
may sinumiti na status of consumer complaints report ang ERC sa ating pong representasyon

**[01:22:23.780] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001577)  
at sinabi nila na mula 2001 hanggang 2026,

**[01:22:28.400] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001578)  
umabot ng 3,093 ang complaints na nasa decided, closed, at terminated category.

**[01:22:36.360] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001579)  
81% ng mga total complaints na matanggap ng ERC.

**[01:22:41.120] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001580)  
ay nasabi nga po na decided, closed, at terminated na.

**[01:22:46.540] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001581)  
Maari ba natin malaman kung ano ang mga considerations na sineset ng ERC

**[01:22:51.360] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001582)  
para masabi na closed, at terminated na ang isang complaint?

**[01:22:56.060] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001583)  
On the average, gano po ba katagal ang inaabot ng isang complaint

**[01:23:00.400] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001584)  
para mapagdesisyonan na closed at terminated na ito?

**[01:23:03.900] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001585)  
So, marami pong factors yan.

**[01:23:06.420] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001586)  
Depende po yan sa complaint.

**[01:23:10.460] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001587)  
Sa complainant.

**[01:23:11.120] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001588)  
Kung na-settle ba po ba yung kanyang complaint, yung kanyang issues.

**[01:23:18.120] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001589)  
Nagkaroon ba ng recalculation doon sa differential billing or disputed billing?

**[01:23:26.360] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001590)  
So, marami pong factors, Madam Speaker, hindi lang po yung isa.

**[01:23:32.080] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001591)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Speaker.

**[01:23:33.560] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001592)  
At nakalatag din sa submission ng ERC na minumungkahin nila ang iba't ibang measures para ma-expedite

**[01:23:41.120] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001593)  
ang resolution ng mga consumer complaints.

**[01:23:44.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001594)  
Saan po naibase ng ERC ang mga mungkahe sa prosesong ito?

**[01:23:49.080] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001595)  
Nagkaroon po ba ng assessment, consultation, kasama ang mga distribution utilities, consumer,

**[01:23:54.940] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001596)  
pagdating sa paghahandle ng mga complaints bago buuin ang mga recommended measures na ito?

**[01:24:01.700] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001597)  
Basically, hindi po kailangan niyong magkaroon ng consultation.

**[01:24:11.120] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001598)  
Trabaho po mismo ng ERC na mag-regulate.

**[01:24:14.060] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001599)  
Ang ginawa lang po ng ERC, paano ba natin papaiksiin yung time frame?

**[01:24:18.520] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001600)  
Huwag natin patagalin, lalo na sa paggawa po ng mga desisyon ng mga complaints ng ating mga kababayan.

**[01:24:24.540] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001601)  
Nabanggit niyo kanina yung mga kakulangan pagdating sa personnel na kasama natin sa pagtitiyak ng mahigpit, malakas na regulasyon.

**[01:24:35.460] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001602)  
Maari ba kayong magbigay ng detalye tungkol dito?

**[01:24:38.440] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001603)  
Well, Madam Speaker,

**[01:24:41.120] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001604)  
highly technical po ang kailangan po natin, even sa mga computations.

**[01:24:46.900] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001605)  
Hindi lang po isang CPA, kaya po yan.

**[01:24:50.120] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001606)  
So, medyo highly technical po ang pagdating sa kuryente.

**[01:24:54.440] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001607)  
We have to balance yung transformer, yung line, yung poste, from genco to transmission to DU to consumers, pilferage, etc.

**[01:25:02.540] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001608)  
So, we need highly technical people to do it.

**[01:25:06.020] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001609)  
And we need also na maging maganda rin po yung sweldo po nila.

**[01:25:11.120] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001610)  
Dahil aminsan ang nangyari, na-train na po ng ERC,

**[01:25:13.900] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001611)  
pero pag may dumating na isang private company na mas maganda yung offer,

**[01:25:19.160] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001612)  
na napapirate pa po yung ating mga magagaling na tao.

**[01:25:22.420] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001613)  
So, you just imagine,

**[01:25:24.080] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001614)  
ang ERC po ay binabantayan trillion of peso industry,

**[01:25:28.420] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001615)  
pero ang pambantay mo, ang pulis mo, 900 million lamang.

**[01:25:32.500] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001616)  
So, ganun po kahirap yung dinadaanan ng ERC.

**[01:25:35.700] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001617)  
If Congress and even Senate, if we do not do something about it,

**[01:25:40.300] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001618)  
if we do not,

**[01:25:41.120] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001619)  
add the budget ng ERC,

**[01:25:43.160] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001620)  
mas mamumroblema po ang taong bayan,

**[01:25:46.240] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001621)  
lalo na kung hindi mababantayan o ma-reregulate ang kuryente dito po sa ating bayan.

**[01:25:51.980] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001622)  
Madam Speaker, nagkaroon din ang pagdinig dito sa Kongreso,

**[01:25:55.900] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001623)  
talakayan tungkol sa rotational brownout sa Visayas.

**[01:26:00.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001624)  
At isa po sa mga hinain ng ating mga consumers ay tungkol halimbawa po sa Iloilo City,

**[01:26:10.700] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001625)  
sa More Power residential rate na nagkaroon po ng pagtaas nitong July 2026,

**[01:26:18.540] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001626)  
mula 13.91 per kilowatt hour tungong 14.349 per kilowatt hour.

**[01:26:26.700] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001627)  
Maari po bang maipaliwanag ito ng ERC?

**[01:26:29.060] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001628)  
Bakit po nagkakaroon pa rin ng mga pagtaas?

**[01:26:32.980] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001629)  
Well, in the Visayas, Madam Speaker, as I've explained a while ago with DOE,

**[01:26:37.700] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001630)  
yung mga planta po talaga nag-breakdown.

**[01:26:42.920] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001631)  
So, naging tight po yung supply.

**[01:26:45.460] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001632)  
But I've explained also, magbubukasan na po yung ating mga planta, may dates na po yan.

**[01:26:51.600] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001633)  
Kaya lang the record is with DOE, now we are tackling ERC.

**[01:26:57.420] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001634)  
But I will give the details to our distinguished colleagues,

**[01:27:04.060] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001635)  
if I may satisfy you with the answer,

**[01:27:07.700] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001636)  
it's not only the problem of ERC, but even DOE.

**[01:27:11.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001637)  
So, they're taking action regarding po sa yellow alert, red alert sa Visayas area.

**[01:27:18.580] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001638)  
Narinig natin na patong-patong po talaga yung pasanin ng ating mga kababayan.

**[01:27:24.980] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001639)  
Bukod po sa pataas na presyo ng langis, na nakaka-apekto rin sa presyo ng kuryente,

**[01:27:30.080] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001640)  
at ngayon, marami din mga pinapasan na iba't-ibang mga fees.

**[01:27:33.700] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001641)  
Kaya, panahon na talaga para ma-review natin.

**[01:27:37.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001642)  
Ang lahat ng mga batas na kaugnay nito.

**[01:27:39.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001643)  
At matiyak din natin na kung tayo po ay mga mekanismo, may mekanismo para sa regulasyon,

**[01:27:45.140] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001644)  
ay mahigpit ito na susuportahan ng ating pamahalaan.

**[01:27:49.340] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001645)  
At higit sa lahat, nabibigyan ng prioridad ang interes ng publiko, ang interes ng ating mga consumers.

**[01:27:55.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001646)  
Maraming maraming salamat, Madam Speaker. Maraming salamat, Ginong Sponsor.

**[01:27:58.540] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-001647)  
Thank you. Majority Leader.

**[01:28:01.540] Rep. Daphne A. Lagon** (asr-001648)  
Madam Speaker, I move to recognize Representative Dagooc of APEC Partylist for his interpellation.

**[01:28:07.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-001649)  
The Honorable Serge Dagooc of Partylist APEC is recognized for his interpolation.

**[01:28:16.520] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001650)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker. Thank you, Majority Leader.

**[01:28:22.520] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001651)  
Magandang gabi po sa ating Energy Regulatory Commission headed by Chairman Nino at sa ating butihing sponsor.

**[01:28:31.520] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001652)  
Ako po ay nahihiya magtanong.

**[01:28:35.520] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001653)  
Kasi dahil sa napakaliit na budget, ang hirap po mag-utos kapag ka...

**[01:28:44.380] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001654)  
I really share the sentiment of the sponsor.

**[01:28:53.140] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001655)  
Paano po natin madisiplina yung ating mga nire-regulate?

**[01:29:05.520] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001656)  
Mas magaling po yung mga tao doon sa planta kaysa taga-ERC.

**[01:29:09.720] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001657)  
So, mahirap po talaga yan.

**[01:29:12.280] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001658)  
Imagine, even the Committee Secretary ng Energy, si Efrain Cortes, nasa generisyo na.

**[01:29:22.260] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001659)  
So, paano?

**[01:29:24.380] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001660)  
So, kaya ilimit ko na lang yung aking mga ano sa mga mangilang proposal.

**[01:29:35.520] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001661)  
And recommendations doon sa mga rules natin.

**[01:29:39.300] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001662)  
Unang-una, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor,

**[01:29:44.980] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001663)  
mayroon po tayong binabalangkas, including the Senate,

**[01:29:49.200] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001664)  
yung removal ng non-technical losses at saka lowering the cap of our...

**[01:30:00.000] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001665)  
So, most probably, ang sinusulong ng Department of Energy, mga 7 billion na ipapautang sa mga particularly electric cooperatives. Kasi alam naman po natin lahat yan sa energy family na hindi mo po maipababa yung technical kung hindi mo gagastusan yung infra mo.

**[01:30:22.320] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001666)  
So, sa tingin po ba ng Energy Regulatory Commission, Distinguished Sponsor, kailangan pa natin mag-create ng special unit para mapabilis yung evaluation and approval ng CAPEX intended for systems loss reduction.

**[01:30:45.740] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001667)  
Very specific po siya, for systems loss reduction program capital expenditures petition.

**[01:30:52.320] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001668)  
Yes, totally agree.

**[01:30:55.500] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001669)  
Thank you, Distinguished Sponsor, Madam Speaker.

**[01:31:00.200] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001670)  
Pangalawa po, siguro the ERC will agree with me na paikutan yung mga rules natin.

**[01:31:11.980] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001671)  
Banggitin ko na sa mga GENCO.

**[01:31:15.120] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001672)  
Kasi upon reviewing the data given to me by NGCP,

**[01:31:22.320] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001673)  
doon sa mga planta na involved sa Ancillary Services Procurement Agreement,

**[01:31:29.780] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001674)  
nakita ko may isang planta, tatlong bisis na ibinta yung ano niya.

**[01:31:37.340] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001675)  
Nakita ko din, I hope I am wrong, but the ERC will evaluate this statement, no?

**[01:31:46.140] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001676)  
Kung ako may planta sa Luzon, Visayas, Mindanao,

**[01:31:50.440] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001677)  
pwede kong patirikin ang planta ko kahit saang isla.

**[01:31:56.940] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001678)  
Dalawa-tatlong planta, halimbawa sampo yung planta ko, patirikin ko yung tatlo.

**[01:32:04.260] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001679)  
Kikita pa rin ako ng mas malaki pa doon sa umaandar yung sampong planta ko.

**[01:32:11.460] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001680)  
Natitipid ko pa yung operating expenses ko sa tatlong planta dahil hindi siya umaandar.

**[01:32:17.620] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001681)  
Anong gagawin ko?

**[01:32:20.440] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001682)  
Doon ko ibibinta sa reserve market.

**[01:32:24.140] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001683)  
So, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor,

**[01:32:28.340] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001684)  
ang tinutukoy ko dito kung may hiwalay ba na price determination methodology

**[01:32:37.440] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001685)  
ang ginagamit sa wholesale electricity spot market at sa reserve market.

**[01:32:43.180] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001686)  
Hiwalay po ba yung rules?

**[01:32:46.000] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001687)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[01:32:49.360] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001688)  
And mas kailangan po.

**[01:32:50.440] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001689)  
Mag-agree po natin siguro ng closer market monitoring para hindi po tayo nagkaka...

**[01:32:56.200] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001690)  
Alam po, alam po niyo po ang sagot.

**[01:32:57.860] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001691)  
Kaya pag nag-retire po si Kong Seryo, pwede po yan sa DOE, pwede rin nga sa ERC.

**[01:33:03.620] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001692)  
Salamat at hindi ko na yun itanong kasi yung sagot mo yan yung...

**[01:33:08.500] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001693)  
Ang itatanong ko na lang, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor,

**[01:33:14.680] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001694)  
mag-aagree ba ang Energy Regulatory Commission

**[01:33:18.200] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001695)  
na malaki yung possibility na tama yung hinala ko?

**[01:33:27.180] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001696)  
Can we...

**[01:33:30.200] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001697)  
Yung sinabi ko kanina, Distinguished Sponsor, na itong planta na ito, na ibinta ganito.

**[01:33:35.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001698)  
So, would the ERC agree with my observation na there is a possibility na pinaikutan nung mga ginko...

**[01:33:48.200] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001700)  
...yung weakness ng mga rules natin?

**[01:33:50.840] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001701)  
Yes, Madam Speaker, in-orderan na po ang PMC ng ERC to investigate on this matter.

**[01:33:58.440] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001702)  
Maganda po yun.

**[01:34:02.380] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001703)  
Makakatulong po yun.

**[01:34:03.240] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001704)  
Pero, again, I would like to reiterate na i-review natin yung rules kasi kung makikita mo yung data,

**[01:34:13.980] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001705)  
lumiliit po yung transaction sa asamble.

**[01:34:18.200] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001706)  
Pero, palaki ng palaki in billions yung transaction sa reserve market.

**[01:34:28.340] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001707)  
Malinaw po na, binabato nila sa reserve market yung kanilang capacity doon sa mga umaandar nilang planta.

**[01:34:39.300] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001708)  
Kasi, tumataas yung ating market price sa reserve market tuwing nagkaroon ng yellow at red alert.

**[01:34:48.200] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001709)  
Kaya, sinabi ko po, kahit hindi umaandar itong planta na ito, yung kabila na umaandar, kumikita ng dubli.

**[01:34:55.500] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001710)  
Kaya, kahit wala itong operasyon, yung revenue nila ay parehas pa din o mas malaki pa.

**[01:35:03.440] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001711)  
Yan po yung tinutukoy ko na pwedeng tingnan ng ERC.

**[01:35:09.300] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001712)  
Yes, we totally agree and review the policy regarding this matter, Madam Speaker.

**[01:35:15.400] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001713)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker.

**[01:35:18.200] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001714)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[01:35:20.900] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001715)  
Doon po sa policy ng ERC, sa deferment, naintindihan ko po na naawa po ang ating regulator dahil sa pagtaas.

**[01:35:34.640] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001716)  
Kaya, nagkaroon tayo ng mga advisory na huwag mo nang singilin, utay-utayin.

**[01:35:41.020] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001717)  
So, doon po sa pag-utay-utay, baka lang po.

**[01:35:48.200] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001718)  
Mayroong hindi nakikita ng ERC na ang working capital ng mga electric cooperatives na maliliit ay hindi po kakayanin yung apat na billing period.

**[01:36:01.620] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001719)  
Ibig sabihin, kung malaking customer ang hindi nagbabayad at dahil maliit lang ang DSM nung electric cooperative na yun,

**[01:36:18.200] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001720)  
at malaki yung component nung generation, the possibility is hindi masahuran yung mga empleyado dyan kasi uunahin niya yung planta para hindi siya maputulan.

**[01:36:32.720] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001721)  
So, po pwede po ba, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor, na i-review ng ERC na very specific distribution utility, yung kanyang orderan,

**[01:36:46.440] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001722)  
dahil makikita naman po niya yung MAPSR, yung Monthly Financial and Statistical Report,

**[01:36:54.000] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001723)  
na itong co-op na ito, maliit lang ang working capital, hindi niya kakayanin ng 4 months, so i-a-allow siya na mas maiksi yung period.

**[01:37:08.160] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001724)  
Tapos, ito dahil yung working capital niya, nakikita sa cash flow statement yan,

**[01:37:14.120] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001725)  
ay medyo mataas yung PC niya, ay ito yung 4 months.

**[01:37:19.640] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001726)  
Can the ERC, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor, consider that?

**[01:37:27.900] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001727)  
Actually, I totally agree with the Congressman Dagoog, but ang ERC po ay nililift na yung no disconnection policy.

**[01:37:41.120] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001728)  
As of October.

**[01:37:44.120] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001729)  
Except for residential areas with 200 kilowatt hour consumption.

**[01:37:49.840] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001730)  
Okay.

**[01:37:50.340] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001731)  
And below.

**[01:37:51.140] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001732)  
Thank you, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[01:37:54.240] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001733)  
Now, doon sa mga requirements natin sa permit sa planta, nare-review na po ba, Distinguished Sponsor, yung mga permit requirements natin?

**[01:38:06.740] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001734)  
Halimbawa, yung mga maliliit na planta.

**[01:38:10.940] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001735)  
Powerblock is the principal author of...

**[01:38:14.120] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001736)  
The Microgrid Act.

**[01:38:16.340] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001737)  
So, mga maliliit na isla yun, na planta.

**[01:38:19.840] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001738)  
Ang requirements, parihos lang sa mga malalaking planta.

**[01:38:24.880] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001739)  
50 kilowatt, 100 kilowatt, natitinga.

**[01:38:29.740] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001740)  
Tapos na, hindi maka-operate dahil mahirap i-comply.

**[01:38:34.240] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001741)  
So, can the ERC review the requirements?

**[01:38:39.580] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001742)  
Then, huwag i-require doon sa mga maliliit yung requirements sa malalaki.

Si...

**[01:40:12.640] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001768)

Chair Muna,

**[01:40:16.140] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001770)  
last kami nagka...

**[01:40:18.840] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001771)

**[01:40:23.620] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001773)

Because I am...

**[01:40:33.340] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001777)

**[01:40:36.440] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001778)

**[01:40:41.020] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001779)

**[01:40:44.120] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001780)

**[01:40:45.160] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001781)

**[01:40:51.540] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001782)

**[01:40:52.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001783)

**[01:40:58.440] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001785)

**[01:41:01.000] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001786)

**[01:41:07.020] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001787)

**[01:41:09.940] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001788)

**[01:41:12.240] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001789)

**[01:41:13.440] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001790)

**[01:41:15.060] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001791)  
We have to be careful

**[01:41:16.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001792)

**[01:41:20.840] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001793)

**[01:41:23.580] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001794)

**[01:41:26.300] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001795)  
Madam Speaker,

**[01:41:27.780] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001796)  
distinguished sponsor, no?

**[01:41:30.240] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001797)  
With all juryspeak,

**[01:41:31.580] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001798)  
kahit hindi ako lawyer,

**[01:41:34.020] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001799)  
ang...

**[01:41:35.180] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001800)

**[01:41:40.800] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001801)  
Kasi, from the beginning,

**[01:41:41.940] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001802)  
hindi po...

**[01:41:42.560] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001803)  
hindi po dapat pinahintulotan ng ERC

**[01:41:45.620] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001804)

**[01:41:51.060] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001805)

**[01:41:54.400] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001806)  
sa National Grid Corporation of the Philippines.

**[01:41:59.480] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001807)  
69 states that

**[01:42:01.140] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001808)  
the grantee

**[01:42:03.560] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001809)  
is liable for a 3% franchise tax

**[01:42:07.820] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001810)  
in liable taxes, period.

**[01:42:10.140] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001811)  
Wala pong dinugtong na sentence na...

**[01:42:12.560] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001812)  
undefranchised tax can be passed on.

**[01:42:15.860] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001813)  
Wala po.

**[01:42:16.800] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001814)  
Doon po nanggaling yung argument ko.

**[01:42:19.880] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001815)  
Kaya nakita po yun ng BIR

**[01:42:21.340] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001816)  
at sinabi ng BIR,

**[01:42:24.280] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001817)  
tama si Kongres Mandaguok,

**[01:42:26.300] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001818)  
hindi pwedeng ipasa.

**[01:42:27.540] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001819)  
Kaya nag-issue si ERC ng Resolution No. 10

**[01:42:29.940] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001820)  
noong 2023,

**[01:42:31.800] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001821)  
nahihinto.

**[01:42:32.900] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001822)  
Kaya,

**[01:42:33.900] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001823)  
maningil tayo ngayon,

**[01:42:36.840] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001824)  
yung nakulikta illegally,

**[01:42:38.620] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001825)  
no?

**[01:42:39.560] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001826)  
Mula noong na-approve yung petition ng NGCP,

**[01:42:42.560] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001827)  
na ipasa.

**[01:42:43.940] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001828)  
So, palagay ko naman po,

**[01:42:45.720] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001829)  
wala pong retroactivity issue.

**[01:42:48.140] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001830)  
I agree with Kongres Mandaguok,

**[01:42:50.380] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001831)  
but the main problem is

**[01:42:52.840] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001832)  
nag-issue po kasi dati ng resolusyon

**[01:42:55.120] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001833)  
ang ERC way back.

**[01:42:57.680] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001834)  
Hindi po pa naon ni Charmona,

**[01:42:59.440] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001835)  
hindi po pa naon ngayon.

**[01:43:00.340] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001836)  
Kaya nga po,

**[01:43:01.180] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001837)  
pinag-aaralan po natin na maigi

**[01:43:03.100] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001838)  
dahil kawawa naman po yung ating pong mga nag-implement.

**[01:43:07.220] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001839)  
Konting mali,

**[01:43:08.000] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001840)  
baka sila po yung makasuhan

**[01:43:09.420] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001841)  
kahit tama na po yung ating ginagawa.

**[01:43:11.100] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001842)  
So, we are just being careful.

**[01:43:12.980] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001843)  
Mr. Speaker, Madam Speaker.

**[01:43:15.200] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001844)  
Kawawa naman po tayo

**[01:43:16.580] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001845)  
kasi pati ako,

**[01:43:17.480] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001846)  
nagbabayad ako ng kuryente.

**[01:43:19.700] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001847)  
Binabayaran ko yung illegally collected na franchise tax.

**[01:43:23.800] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001848)  
Tapos, pinatungan pa ng VAT.

**[01:43:25.620] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001849)  
Tax na ngayon.

**[01:43:27.180] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001850)  
Tapos, tinax ulit.

**[01:43:28.460] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001851)  
So, anyway.

**[01:43:32.780] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001852)  
Ganon na lang ang mga katanungan ko.

**[01:43:35.420] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001853)  
Diyan na lang po, Madam Speaker.

**[01:43:37.380] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001854)  
Maraming salamat sa pagsagot,

**[01:43:38.980] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001855)  
Distinguished Sponsor.

**[01:43:40.200] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-001856)  
Maraming salamat, ERC.

Thank you.

Majority Leader.

for his interpellation.

**[01:43:55.540] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001863)

**[01:43:58.600] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001864)
