**[01:44:00.240] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001865)  
I would like to,

**[01:44:01.560] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001866)  
this presentation,

**[01:44:02.620] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001867)  
I would like to ask

**[01:44:03.660] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001868)  
if the sponsor is willing to answer

**[01:44:06.300] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001869)  
some interpellations.

**[01:44:12.560] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001870)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[01:44:15.400] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001871)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker.

**[01:44:17.380] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001872)  
Madam Speaker, tungkol po ito sa

**[01:44:19.340] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001873)  
line loss congestion charge.

**[01:44:22.660] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001874)  
Yung line loss congestion charge,

**[01:44:24.800] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001875)  
ang cooperative,

**[01:44:27.660] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001876)  
electric cooperatives

**[01:44:28.580] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001877)  
ng Region 8

**[01:44:29.600] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001878)  
at saka Region 6

**[01:44:31.580] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001879)  
ay

**[01:44:33.340] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001880)  
nagbibili sa WESM

**[01:44:35.720] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001881)  
at

**[01:44:38.340] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001882)  
kung ang nabibili nila

**[01:44:40.360] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001883)  
ay galing Luzon na

**[01:44:41.700] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001884)  
GENCO

**[01:44:43.900] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001885)  
generating company.

**[01:44:47.900] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001886)  
At nagkakakongestiyon

**[01:44:50.200] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001887)  
dyan sa

**[01:44:51.100] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001888)  
Luzon grid.

**[01:44:54.560] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001889)  
Hindi na ipapadala yung kuryente

**[01:44:56.380] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001890)  
sa Region 6 at saka Region 8

**[01:44:58.400] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001891)  
pero

**[01:45:00.000] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001892)  
Sinacharge pa rin ang electric cooperatives. Nung tinignan ko po yung billing sa Eastern Summit, yung 13 pesos ay umaabot ng 18 pesos.

**[01:45:17.120] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001893)  
At kung ang hindi nagbayad na electric cooperative ay sisingilin, aabot po ng 25 pesos per kilowatt hour ang babayaran po.

**[01:45:32.360] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001894)  
Napaka-unfair po ito dahil hindi naman ito nagamit ng consumers. Hindi ito dumating sa Region 8 at sa Region 6. At napag-usapan din po namin ito nung pre-budget briefing.

**[01:45:47.120] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001895)  
At nung August 13, 2026, nagbigay ng order ang ERC to adopt an interim measure suspending collection payment of LRTA for trading intervals where different system marginal prices arise because of congestion.

**[01:46:09.340] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001896)  
The suspension is not blanket. It applies only when the conditions specified by ERC exist.

**[01:46:17.120] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001897)  
Tama po ba?

**[01:46:19.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001898)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[01:46:22.320] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001899)  
Madam Speaker, yung...

**[01:46:25.020] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001900)  
If I may add, Minority Leader, kung kailan po nag-arise yung problema, kaya nga po nag-order yung ating ERC nung August 13, 2026 sa IMAP to recalculate the allocation of the net settlement surplus.

**[01:46:44.300] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001901)  
Even nung simula.

**[01:46:46.240] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001902)  
So hindi lang po yung...

**[01:46:47.120] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001903)  
Simula pa nung June 2021.

**[01:46:50.440] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001904)  
At kung makita po, basing doon sa investigation, kung may kailangan pong ibalik na pera,

**[01:46:55.920] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001905)  
i-re-rebate rin po yun sa mga consumers po natin na yun po yung concern na ni Minority Leader Limanan.

**[01:47:06.860] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001906)  
Maraming salamat po, Madam Speaker, sa answer.

**[01:47:09.080] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001907)  
Pero ang IMOP ay sumulat pa rin sa isa mail ko na hindi nila...

**[01:47:17.120] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001908)  
na hindi nila nire-recognize yata yung suspension.

**[01:47:19.300] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001909)  
Dahil suspension lang ito eh.

**[01:47:22.360] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001910)  
For 30 days.

**[01:47:24.400] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001911)  
E paano po ito?

**[01:47:25.520] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001912)  
Sabi ko nga, pag-implement ito, 25 pesos per kilowatt hour ng babayaran, parang papatay na natin ang mga consumers.

**[01:47:34.000] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001913)  
I think dalawa po yun.

**[01:47:36.240] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001914)  
So different po.

**[01:47:37.080] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001915)  
Yung bago pong order ng ating pong ERC is effective September ngayon po.

**[01:47:47.120] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001916)  
Pero yung sinasabi niyo po sa IMOP na kausap pa yung pinu-collect ng IMOP sa mail ko,

**[01:47:54.760] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001917)  
prior month pa po yun.

**[01:47:58.380] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001918)  
So this is a new order, Madam Speaker, na kailangan pong sundin ng IMOP regarding sa computation.

**[01:48:08.940] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001919)  
So we have to recalculate.

**[01:48:10.260] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001920)  
Hindi lang po ngayon, but way back 2021, because the chairman...

**[01:48:17.120] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001921)  
ERC mismo, naiintindihan po yung sitwasyon ng ating mga kababayan sa distrito po ng minority leader.

**[01:48:26.020] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001922)  
So they have to follow.

**[01:48:28.600] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001923)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[01:48:29.520] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001924)  
Madam Speaker.

**[01:48:30.560] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001925)  
Anyway, Madam Speaker, magkano na ba ang total amount na nakulekta mula June 2021 ng IMOP at pinaghati-hatian ng mga GINCOs?

**[01:48:43.480] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001926)  
Yes, Madam Speaker, if I may, can you give us...

**[01:48:47.120] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001927)  
At the same time, we will ask IMOP to submit yung kanilang calculation to the ERC and will be given to the minority leader.

**[01:48:55.920] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001928)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker.

**[01:48:57.800] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001929)  
Madam Speaker, may principle tayo in civil law na tinatawag na solutio indebiti, unjust enrichment.

**[01:49:09.040] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001930)

**[02:06:14.540] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002161)  
the Committee on Rules and the leadership

**[02:06:16.940] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002162)

**[02:06:18.380] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002163)

**[02:06:24.760] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002166)

**[02:06:26.260] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002167)

**[02:06:28.680] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002168)

**[02:06:31.440] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002170)

**[02:06:34.100] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002172)

**[02:06:35.840] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002173)

**[02:06:37.940] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002174)

**[02:06:41.500] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002176)  
Honorable Chanko, applies to members

**[02:06:43.780] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002177)

**[02:06:45.140] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002178)

**[02:06:47.680] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002179)

**[02:06:50.120] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002180)

**[02:06:51.500] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002181)

**[02:06:53.880] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002182)

**[02:06:55.980] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002183)

**[02:06:58.680] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002184)  
Yeah, session suspended.

**[02:07:01.340] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002185)  
So, just to align,

**[02:07:03.440] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002186)  
just for the record,

**[02:07:04.680] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002187)  
we will just validate the name of your staff,

**[02:07:06.620] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002188)  
we will confer with the rules on what happened

**[02:07:08.460] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002189)  
and then we will resume session.

**[02:07:10.660] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002190)  
Session suspended for a few minutes.

**[02:07:11.940] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002191)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[02:13:56.580] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002207)  
Session resumed.

**[02:13:58.680] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002208)  
After discussion with the Secretariat of the Rules Committee,

**[02:14:06.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002209)  
the ruling of the presiding officer says,

**[02:14:10.500] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002210)  
all congressional staff with valid ID are allowed to take pictures and photos of their principal

**[02:14:19.080] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002211)  
as long as they will not disrupt the plenary.

**[02:14:23.080] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002212)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker.

**[02:14:24.600] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002213)  
So, does it mean to say, papayagan na pong pumasok yung staff ko right now?

**[02:14:27.860] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002214)  
Yes, they will be admitted as long as they present their official ID

**[02:14:32.300] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002215)  
and that they're plantilia staff of the members of Congress.

**[02:14:35.380] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002216)  
Okay, pangalawa pong parliamentary inquiry, Madam Speaker.

**[02:14:40.100] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002217)  
I'm glad that you're the one presiding today.

**[02:14:42.560] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002218)  
Noong pong lunes, you called me out of order because of section 105.

**[02:14:50.540] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002219)  
Ang sabi niyo po doon, I cannot request to reopen the budget

**[02:14:56.780] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002220)  
of the house because

**[02:15:00.000] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002221)  
At ang sinayot niyo po noon is when a measure, report, or motion is approved, adopted, or lost, a member who voted, this is the important word, who voted with the majority may move for its reconsideration on the same or succeeding session.

**[02:15:28.220] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002222)  
Ang sabi niyo po noon, since wala po ako doon, hindi ako bumoto, kaya hindi ako pwede mag-reconsider.

**[02:15:36.760] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002223)  
But this section should be related to this section on voting. It cannot stand alone.

**[02:15:46.300] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002224)  
And what does the voting say? Voting says we vote either ace or nace or nominal voting.

**[02:15:54.600] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002225)  
There are two modes of voting. Ace and nace.

**[02:15:58.220] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002226)  
And nominal voting. So hanapin ko lang po kung ano yung exactong rule.

**[02:16:07.360] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002227)  
Rule 16, Section 117, Manner of Voting. So hindi ko nang buhahabain, papahabain, no?

**[02:16:17.440] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002228)  
The speaker shall arise and state the motion or request that is being put to vote in a clear, precise, simple language.

**[02:16:24.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002229)  
The speaker shall say as many as in favor as the question may be, say aye.

**[02:16:34.260] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002230)  
After the affirmative vote is counted, the speaker shall say as many as opposed, say nay.

**[02:16:41.460] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002231)  
Sumunod po noon, nominal voting. Hindi ko nababasahin, hindi effecto.

**[02:16:45.960] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002232)  
Ang sabi niyo po doon, kaya hindi po pwedeng pagbigyan yung reconsideration ko, is because,

**[02:16:54.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002233)  
I did not vote. Okay?

**[02:16:58.040] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002234)  
I will make it clear, and gusto niyo balikan natin yung video, hindi po umabot sa voting.

**[02:17:04.980] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002235)  
Meron lang pong nag-motion to terminate, merong nag-agree, pero hindi po umabot sa voting.

**[02:17:10.680] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002236)  
At hindi niyo po pwede idahilan na dahil walang tumutol, ay hindi na umabot sa voting.

**[02:17:17.060] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002237)  
Because, pag second reading, when you request for the approval of the measure, even if no one objects,

**[02:17:24.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002238)  
you go through voting.

**[02:17:27.900] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002239)  
So dito, hindi umabot sa voting, hindi applicable po yung sinasabi niyong section. Maliwanag po yan.

**[02:17:37.140] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002240)  
In response to the query of the Hon. Chanko, if I may,

**[02:17:42.760] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002241)  
Section 105 refers to the motion for reconsideration as put forward by any member of this House.

**[02:17:50.040] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002242)  
The presiding officer stands by a ruling,

**[02:17:54.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002243)  
that a motion for reconsideration shall only be allowed to a member who voted with the majority.

**[02:18:02.420] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002244)  
With regard to your query whether an ayes and nays or a nominal voting was conducted,

**[02:18:08.620] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002245)  
the ayes and nays is done when we approve a measure on second reading.

**[02:18:13.080] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002246)  
In this case, it was a motion from the minority jointly done with the majority to terminate the period of interpolation.

**[02:18:21.920] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002247)  
In other words, we are on the period of

**[02:18:24.200] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002248)  
sponsorship debate and interpellation.

**[02:18:26.760] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002249)  
And motions with regards to this are done by basic motions, not with an ayes and nays.

**[02:18:32.880] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002250)  
No, but Madam Speaker, Section 105 refers to voting.

**[02:18:37.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002251)  
So the question is, nagdaroon ba ng...

**[02:18:39.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002252)  
Section 105 refers to motions for reconsideration.

**[02:18:41.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002253)  
Yes, with regards to voting.

**[02:18:43.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002254)  
Tignan niyo po yung section.

**[02:18:45.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002255)  
If I may, Your Honor, allow me to read Section 105.

**[02:18:51.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002256)  
When a measure, report, or motion is approved,

**[02:18:55.000] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002259)  
adopted or lost,

**[02:18:57.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002261)  
and in this case, it's a motion to approve the motion to terminate the period of interpolation

**[02:19:04.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002262)  
and the proposed budget of a certain agency.

**[02:19:07.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002263)  
Yes, was there voting?

**[02:19:09.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002264)  
Yes, because the presiding officer said, is there any objection?

**[02:19:15.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002265)  
It's a motion to terminate the period of interpolation and the proposed budget

**[02:19:23.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002266)  
of whichever agency was discussed.

**[02:19:26.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002267)  
Yes, pero sa pag nagbubotohan tayo ng second reading, kahit walang mag-object,

**[02:19:31.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002268)  
you still call the eisen-nace.

**[02:19:33.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002269)  
That is for second reading, Honorable Chanko.

**[02:19:36.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002270)  
Okay, if you will insist, Madam Speaker, let us go by your theory.

**[02:19:40.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002271)  
I am present here today.

**[02:19:42.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002272)  
With due courtesy, Honorable Chanko.

**[02:19:44.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002273)  
It is not my theory, it is the rules of the House of Representatives,

**[02:19:48.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002274)  
and I'm citing Section 105, which refers to motions for reconsideration.

**[02:19:52.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002275)  
It is your interpretation of the rules.

**[02:19:55.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002276)  
It is not my interpretation, it is the rules,

**[02:19:59.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002277)  
and as presiding officer, that is what we stand for.

**[02:20:02.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002278)  
Okay, sige ha, sundan natin yung list.

**[02:20:06.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002279)  
With due courtesy, Your Honor, actually what you could have done

**[02:20:09.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002280)  
was that when the presiding officer ruled you out of order,

**[02:20:13.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002281)  
you could have appealed, and we were waiting for you to appeal,

**[02:20:17.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002282)  
then you will be given five seconds to explain your appeal.

**[02:20:20.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002283)  
Five minutes, Madam Speaker.

**[02:20:21.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002284)  
Pardon me, Your Honor?

**[02:20:22.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002285)  
Five minutes, not five seconds.

**[02:20:24.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002286)  
Five minutes, I stand corrected.

**[02:20:26.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002287)  
And after that appeal, if your appeal will be lost, then we can put it into a vote.

**[02:20:30.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002288)  
Unfortunately, during that time, you did not appeal and waived that right.

**[02:20:36.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002289)  
Okay, yes, Madam Speaker, I will explain why I did not appeal at that time.

**[02:20:40.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002290)  
Pinabayaan ko nila lang yun eh.

**[02:20:42.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002291)  
Kasi kahit naman mag-appeal ako, alam naman natin dito, sagasaan dito.

**[02:20:46.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002292)  
Hindi naman ako mananalo sa appeal.

**[02:20:48.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002293)  
Pero sobra na yung nangyayari.

**[02:20:49.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002294)

**[02:20:50.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002295)  
Yung staff ko kanina hindi pinapasok.

**[02:20:52.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002296)  
Nung nagbilangan tayo ng boto dito, pinalabas nila 221.

**[02:20:56.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002297)  
Eh wala pa nga ang kalahat yung nakaupo dito. Eh sobra na.

**[02:20:59.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002298)  
Okay, I will go with your reasoning.

**[02:21:01.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002299)  
Let me correct that for the record, Your Honor.

**[02:21:03.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002300)  
After conferring with our staff,

**[02:21:06.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002301)  
we were informed that they deny the allegation na hindi pinapasok ang staff mo.

**[02:21:14.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002302)  
But the allegation that they were not allowed

**[02:21:18.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002303)  
to take photos and videos was because of the internal agreement or internal rules.

**[02:21:23.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002304)  
That's why you have those white placards or how do you call that?

**[02:21:29.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002305)  
Signages in the gallery.

**[02:21:31.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002306)  
No videos, pictures taken or whatsoever.

**[02:21:34.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002307)  
But the chair has already ruled that that will be allowed as long as they are official,

**[02:21:39.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002308)  
plantilia, staff members of the office, of your office.

**[02:21:45.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002309)  
Okay, let's go back.

**[02:21:46.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002310)  
Pinalampas ko na lang yun. Pero...

**[02:21:48.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002311)  
Just for the record, can we request the Secretary General

**[02:21:51.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002312)  
to retrieve the 200 plus that the Honorable Chanko is referring to?

**[02:21:54.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002313)  
Okay.

**[02:21:55.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002314)  
Is this in relation to your...

**[02:21:57.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002315)  
No, it was in relation to the Speaker calling for a roll call at that time. Okay?

**[02:22:03.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002316)  
Yes, we have the record.

**[02:22:07.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002317)  
It includes 21 members who were present at the Senate Impeachment Court.

**[02:22:12.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002318)  
Yes.

**[02:22:13.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002319)  
It also included members who were on official business similar to the time that

**[02:22:17.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002320)  
during the past weeks when you were on official business since you were in Italy,

**[02:22:22.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002321)  
your presence has also been recorded as present because you were on official business.

**[02:22:28.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002322)  
Okay, okay, Madam Speaker.

**[02:22:30.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002323)  
Let us just go with your ruling.

**[02:22:31.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002324)  
Let us go with your ruling.

**[02:22:33.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002325)  
Madam Speaker, if that is your ruling, I was present today.

**[02:22:37.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002326)  
I voted with the approval of all agencies tackled today.

**[02:22:42.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002327)  
So if we go by your ruling, do you mean to say I can move now to reconsider all these?

**[02:22:46.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002328)  
Do you want to test your ruling? Let's test it now.

**[02:22:50.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002329)  
Would you like to reconsider the proposed...

**[02:22:53.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002330)  
All, all, all, even those in the morning.

**[02:22:55.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002331)  
If that is your ruling, if that is your ruling, then we can test your ruling, Madam Speaker.

**[02:22:59.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002332)  
We are actually tackling the proposed budget on a per-agency basis.

**[02:23:05.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002333)  
Madam Speaker, but you said per day.

**[02:23:07.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002334)  
Madam Speaker, but you said per day.

**[02:23:09.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002335)  
Madam Speaker, you said per day.

**[02:23:10.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002336)  
Hence, if there is no objection, the period of interpolation for that proposed agency

**[02:23:15.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002337)  
is terminated.

**[02:23:17.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002338)  
But if the Honorable Chanko is so willing to request for the reopening of any agency

**[02:23:24.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002339)  
to which he was present, then we will consider that.

**[02:23:27.520] Rep. Tobias "Toby" M. Tiangco** (asr-002340)  
Okay.

**[02:23:28.520] Rep. Aniela Bianca D. Tolentino** (asr-002341)  
Madam Speaker, I move to suspend session for today.

**[02:23:30.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002342)  
Session suspended.

**[03:34:32.470] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002505)  
Session resumed. Majority Leader, please.

**[03:35:00.000] Rep. Aniela Bianca D. Tolentino** (asr-002506)  
motion for reconsideration is out of order. I so move, Mr. Speaker.

**[03:35:04.120] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002506-split-2)

**[03:35:23.680] Rep. Aniela Bianca D. Tolentino** (asr-002506-split-3)

**[03:35:25.760] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002506-split-4)

**[03:37:30.000] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-002511)  
Mr. Speaker, colleagues, I rise to sponsor the proposed fiscal year 2027 budget of the Civil Service Commission, including the Career Executive Service Board. As the central human resource agency of the government, the CSC carries a constitutional mandate that goes to the very heart of effective governance,

**[03:38:00.000] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-002512)  
ensuring that appointments and personnel actions in the public service are guided by merit and fitness, while promoting professionalism, integrity, efficiency, responsiveness, and accountability among our public servants. The proposed fiscal year 2027 budget of 4.04 billion pesos, including automatic appropriations,

**[03:38:31.880] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-002513)  
will support the Commission's core functions, including human resource governance, professionalization in the workplace, [unclear] and administrative justice, as well as the continuing Philippine Civil Service Modernization Project, which will support the digital conversion of HR systems, records and [unclear].

**[03:39:00.000] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-002514)  
The Career Executive Service Board, meanwhile, is proposing a budget of 142.86 million. The Board plays an important role in developing and maintaining a competent executive service that provides continuity, professionalism, [unclear] leadership in government. With this consideration,

**[03:39:30.000] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-002515)  
I respectfully present the proposed

**[03:39:32.000] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-002516)  
fiscal year 2027 budget of the

**[03:39:34.000] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-002517)  
Civil Service Commission, including

**[03:39:36.000] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-002518)

for the consideration of

**[03:39:40.000] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-002520)  
this honorable body.

**[03:40:32.800] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002534)

**[03:40:52.620] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002535)

**[03:41:04.660] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002536)

**[03:41:22.620] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002537)  
i-undertake para po hindi ma-upend yung civil service tungkol dito. **[03:41:52.620] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-002538)

**[03:42:01.200] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002539)

**[03:42:02.800] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-002540)

**[03:42:16.940] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-002541)  
Sir Speaker,

**[03:42:18.220] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-002542)

**[03:42:32.560] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-002543)

**[03:42:42.100] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-002544)

**[03:42:48.220] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-002546)

**[03:43:43.400] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002552)

**[03:43:46.740] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002553)

**[03:44:08.240] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-002556)

**[03:44:53.280] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002561)

**[03:44:57.720] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-002562)

**[03:58:22.520] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002666)  
Mr. Speaker, and our esteemed Majority Leader, my dear colleagues, please allow me to acknowledge my co-sponsor.

**[03:58:32.540] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002668)  
Honorable Reynante Arogancia of the 3rd District of Quezon.

**[03:58:38.340] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002669)  
Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleagues, I rise today, I sit today to sponsor the proposed budget of the Department of Transportation for Fiscal Year 2027.

**[03:58:55.180] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002670)  
Every day, millions of Filipinos spend hours in traffic, waiting in long lines at terminals,

**[03:59:02.520] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002671)  
and endure uncertain commutes just to get to work, to school, or home to their families.

**[03:59:12.160] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002672)  
Transportation is not merely about infrastructure.

**[03:59:16.600] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002673)  
It is about time, opportunity, and dignity of our people, for our people.

**[03:59:24.940] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002674)  
This budget responds to that reality.

**[03:59:28.660] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002675)  
Through strategic investments in modern railways,

**[03:59:32.520] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002676)  
safer roads, efficient airports, and stronger maritime facilities,

**[03:59:38.940] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002677)  
we are building a transport system that eases congestion,

**[03:59:43.400] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002678)  
improves safety, and connects communities across our archipelago,

**[03:59:48.620] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002679)  
from the busiest urban centers to our most remote islands.

**[03:59:57.560] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002680)  
A reliable transport networks moves more

**[04:00:00.400] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002681)  
people and goods. It moves the economy forward. It opens markets for our farmers and fisherfolk, brings jobs closer to our workers, and ensures that no region is left behind in our pursuit of growth.

**[04:00:17.620] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002682)  
Mr. Speaker, supporting this budget therefore means supporting mobility, productivity, and inclusive development for every Filipino.

**[04:00:26.740] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002683)  
It is an investment not only in steel, concrete, and runways, but in the future of our nation.

**[04:00:36.400] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002684)  
It is with this conviction that I respectfully urge this August Chamber to approve the proposed budget of the Department of Transportation for the fiscal year 2027.

**[04:00:50.000] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002685)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Speaker, dear colleagues.

**[04:03:23.980] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002717)

**[04:03:26.740] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002718)

**[04:03:35.180] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002719)

**[04:03:39.700] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002720)

**[04:03:44.460] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002721)  
Mayroon pong 600M na nakalaan para sa mga public utility vehicles

**[04:03:50.860] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002722)

**[04:03:56.720] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002723)

**[04:04:00.340] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002724)

**[04:04:07.260] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002725)

**[04:04:12.360] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002726)

**[04:04:19.480] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002727)  
Distinguished Colleague, Mr. Speaker.

**[04:04:22.480] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002728)

**[04:04:24.760] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002729)  
So, sa makatawid.

**[04:04:26.720] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002730)  
Hindi naman po pala totoo na wala tayong subsidy for 2027.

**[04:04:33.920] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002731)

**[04:04:36.300] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002732)

**[04:04:39.960] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002733)

**[04:04:45.680] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002734)

**[04:04:49.320] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002735)

**[04:04:56.060] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002736)  
And,

**[04:04:56.720] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002737)

**[04:05:00.040] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002738)

**[04:05:03.960] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002739)  
1B for marine-based.

**[04:05:07.900] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002740)

**[04:05:13.800] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002741)

**[04:05:20.420] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002742)

Mayroon po.

**[04:05:25.920] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002744)  
Okay.

**[04:05:26.180] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002745)

**[04:05:26.720] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002746)  
Sa 2027, 1.7 billion po nakalaan.

**[04:05:30.940] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002747)  
Thank you, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[04:05:33.640] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002748)  
Ang susunod ko pong katanungan, mayroon po tayong iba-ibang mode of transportation.

**[04:05:45.380] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002749)  
Pwede ko po bang matanong ang aking kaibigan na sponsor,

**[04:05:51.480] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002750)  
ilan po ba talaga yung mode of transportation natin sa buong bansa?

**[04:05:56.720] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002751)  
Land, air, marine, and rail po.

**[04:06:00.640] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002752)

**[04:06:08.380] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002753)

**[04:06:20.800] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002754)

**[04:06:24.720] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002755)

**[04:06:26.720] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002756)

**[04:06:37.280] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002757)

Salamat po, Distinguished Colleague.

**[04:06:50.800] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002759)

**[04:06:55.240] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002760)  
Halos.

**[04:06:55.680] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002761)

**[04:07:04.120] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002762)

**[04:07:10.600] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002763)

**[04:07:15.400] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002764)  
Yan po ay yung unang tatlong pinakamalaking pondo na ilaan para sa DOTR.

**[04:07:22.440] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002765)  
So, Distinguished Sponsor,

**[04:07:25.020] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002766)  
mayroon na po tayong

**[04:07:26.100] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002769)  
maritime subsidy. Magkano po yan?

**[04:07:30.180] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002769-split-2)

**[04:07:42.880] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002769-split-3)

**[04:07:55.680] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002771)

**[04:08:10.260] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002772)  
Ang problema lang po, hindi po naisama sa NEP, pero yan po yung proposal ng DOTR.

**[04:08:16.260] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002773)

**[04:08:19.740] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002774)

**[04:08:26.040] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002775)  
Program.

**[04:08:27.100] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002776)  
[unclear] ko po naitanong 'yan, Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[04:08:32.900] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002777)

**[04:08:38.840] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002778)

**[04:08:46.420] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002779)

**[04:08:53.040] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002781)

**[04:09:03.680] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002783)

**[04:09:09.360] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002784)

**[04:09:19.340] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002786)

**[04:09:23.640] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002787)

**[04:09:27.340] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002788)

**[04:09:40.560] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002789)  
Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleague, tama po yan.

Contracting program.

**[04:09:56.140] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002793)

**[04:10:00.780] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002794)

Distinguished colleague.

**[04:10:17.400] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002799)

**[04:10:20.360] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002800)

**[04:12:52.560] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002819)

**[04:12:53.640] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002820)

**[04:12:59.220] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002821)

**[04:13:01.700] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002822)  
So, medyo may problema tayo sa DBM.

**[04:13:06.420] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002823)

**[04:13:17.200] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002824)

**[04:13:23.640] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002825)

**[04:13:28.460] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002826)

**[04:13:34.780] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002827)

**[04:13:39.020] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002828)

**[04:13:42.260] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002829)

Kasi Pilipino...

**[04:13:53.640] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002831)

**[04:13:58.780] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002832)  
Samart, mga Pilipino din po kami.

**[04:14:00.880] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002833)  
So, anyway, siguro malinaw yung naiparating natin sa DBM.

**[04:14:06.460] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002834)  
Hindi po ito kasalaanan ni DOTR.

**[04:14:09.260] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002835)  
Last question na lang.

**[04:14:11.240] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002836)  
Ganito kakapal yung question ko pero last question na lang ito.

**[04:14:16.300] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002837)  
Doon sa ano natin, sa air transportation.

**[04:14:21.380] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002838)  
In one of the hearings, yata.

**[04:14:23.640] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002839)  
Sa Committee on Transportation, nabanggit ninyo na hindi na sinisingil ni CAB at saka ni CAB yung mga fees and charges sa ating mga airlines.

**[04:14:39.280] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002840)  
Tama po ba yun?

**[04:14:49.740] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002841)  
Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleague.

**[04:14:52.040] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002842)  
Ang tinatanong po nyo is the fuel surcharge.

**[04:14:53.640] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002843)  
Isa po yan.

**[04:14:57.580] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002844)  
Pero may mga fees pa yata yun na...

**[04:15:00.000] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002845)  
Yes, may trigger mechanism po ang fuel surcharge kung nagkaroon po ng 20% increase dito po sa fuel prices, sa global fuel prices po bago po maka-charge po ng fuel surcharge.

**[04:15:16.380] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002846)  
So, tinatanggal po siya, sinususpend pag maabot yung trigger?

**[04:15:21.560] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002847)  
Sir, nagkakaroon po ng instead na 30-day price interval, nagiging 15 days po. So, twice a month po yung pagsukat po ng increase ng fuel prices po.

**[04:15:38.480] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002848)  
Thank you, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[04:15:39.920] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002849)  
But there's an automatic mechanism na pagka bumaba below 100 ang Jet A1 fuel na price, bumaba po ng $100 per barrel, babalik po sa dating 30-day interval.

**[04:15:57.800] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002850)  
So, para lang may idea ang ating mga air passengers, magkano po ba yung fuel surcharge na yan doon sa ticket natin?

**[04:16:11.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002851)

**[04:16:17.820] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002852)

**[04:16:21.560] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002853)

**[04:16:37.120] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002854)

**[04:16:47.820] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002855)  
Yes, please.

**[04:16:48.820] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002856)  
Pero yung pinakamataas.

**[04:16:51.560] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002857)

**[04:16:53.620] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002858)

**[04:17:06.120] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002859)

**[04:17:15.440] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002860)

**[04:17:18.620] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002861)  
So, ang katanungan ko na lang.

**[04:17:21.560] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002862)

**[04:17:25.120] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002863)

**[04:17:39.800] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002864)

**[04:17:44.160] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002865)

**[04:17:51.560] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002866)

**[04:18:05.080] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002867)

**[04:18:16.760] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002868)  
Online.

**[04:18:17.279] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002868-split-2)  
Online. Online. Nakikita nila.

**[04:18:19.080] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-002869)

**[04:18:19.680] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002870)

**[04:18:21.560] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002871)

**[04:18:45.020] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-002874)

**[04:48:11.320] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003252)

**[04:48:19.360] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003253)  
So, ang budget history nitong Metro Manila.

**[04:48:22.040] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003254)  
Metro Manila Subway was, way back in 2024, there was funding for this particular project in the amount of Php 68.4 billion.

**[04:48:31.440] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003255)  
In 2025, Php 7.8 billion.

**[04:48:34.440] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003256)  
In 2026, Php 20.38 billion.

**[04:48:39.260] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003257)  
And sa NEP natin ngayon for 2027, Php 67.44 billion or roughly a 230.8% increase.

**[04:48:49.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003258)  
So, kagaya ng karibigan.

**[04:48:52.040] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003259)  
Ang katanungan ko, ang concern ko rin dito, like for example, the Php 67.442 billion allocation under the NEP 2027, Php 26.71 billion comes from loan proceeds, kung tama yung aking numero.

**[04:49:10.980] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003260)

**[04:49:16.520] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003261)

**[04:49:22.440] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003263)  
It must correspond to specific 2027 contractual milestones, civil works, tunnel stations, depot work, railway system, rolling stock, consulting services, right-of-way acquisition ulit, and related counterpart obligations.

**[04:57:53.980] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003368)

**[04:59:11.800] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003381)

**[04:59:16.920] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003382)  
yung points dun sa data na nakuha ko, Valenzuela to Malolos, partial service, December 2027, Malolos to Clark,

**[04:59:23.340] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003383)

**[05:14:25.260] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003810)  
Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor.

**[05:14:27.260] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003811)  
Before I proceed

**[05:14:29.260] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003812)  
with my questions,

**[05:14:31.260] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003813)  
I wish to first manifest my

**[05:14:33.260] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003814)  
support for the Philippine Coast

**[05:14:35.260] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003815)  
Guard's budget.

**[05:14:37.260] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003816)  
Mandato ng PCG na tiyakin

**[05:14:39.260] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003817)  
ng kaligtasan sa karagatan,

**[05:14:41.260] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003818)  
magpatupad ng batas maritime,

**[05:14:43.260] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003819)  
protektahan ang marine

**[05:14:45.260] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003820)  
environment, at siguraduhin

**[05:14:47.260] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003821)  
ang kaligtasan ng

**[05:14:49.260] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003822)  
paglalayag. Sa West

**[05:14:51.260] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003823)  
Philippine Sea, sila ang nasa

**[05:14:53.260] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003824)  
front line sa pagbabantayan ng ating

**[05:14:55.260] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003825)  
sovereign rights,

**[05:14:57.260] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003826)  
pagprotekta sa ating mga mangingisda,

**[05:14:59.260] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003827)  
at

**[05:15:00.000] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003828)  
pagpapanatili ng lawful at peaceful maritime order.

**[05:15:04.200] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003829)  
Insya Allah, naway ang kanilang budget ay sapat upang patuloy nilang magampanan ang tungkulin,

**[05:15:11.440] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003830)  
lalo na sa harap ng sunod-sunod na panghihimasok ng China sa ating karagatan.

**[05:15:17.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003831)  
Ang kanilang tapang at dedikasyon ay patunay ng ating pambansang naninindigan

**[05:15:23.660] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003832)  
na ipagtanggol ang ating maritime domain at ang ating mamamayan.

**[05:15:28.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003833)  
Mr. Speaker, Good Sponsor, I will now proceed with some questions regarding Marina's role in maritime safety.

**[05:15:38.580] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003834)  
Under the 2027 NEP, Marina has a P904 million budget for the Maritime Industry Regulatory and Supervision Program.

**[05:15:49.640] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003835)  
Mr. Sponsor, maari bang i-elaborate kung ano-ano ang mga sakop ng programang ito?

**[05:15:55.740] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003836)  
Kasama ba rito ang patungkol sa

**[05:15:58.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003837)  
vessel safety and inspection?

**[05:16:01.680] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003838)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[05:16:04.760] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003839)  
ang P904 million over ship and shipyard registration,

**[05:16:10.380] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003840)  
safe areas documentation and certification,

**[05:16:14.220] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003841)  
accreditation and monitoring of safe areas education and training programs

**[05:16:19.020] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003842)  
in ship safety regulatory and enforcement, Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague.

**[05:16:25.780] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003843)  
If vessel safety and inspection,

**[05:16:28.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003844)  
inspection is included, how much is the specific allocation po?

**[05:16:33.280] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003845)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague, total of 61.268 million for ship inspection and enforcement.

**[05:16:42.740] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003846)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague.

**[05:16:45.240] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003847)  
Distinguished Sponsor, as of date, ilang vessels ang na-deny ng certification o na-suspend dahil sa safety deficiencies?

**[05:16:55.220] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003848)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague, MARINA will provide

**[05:16:58.300] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003849)  
information as well as require the regional offices to submit data to your office.

**[05:17:04.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003850)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[05:17:05.360] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003851)  
Can Marina provide data regarding this including the particular violations that resulted in denial or suspension?

**[05:17:14.180] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003852)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague, the same.

**[05:17:16.960] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003853)  
MARINA will also provide information as well as require

**[05:17:20.680] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003854)  
the regional offices to submit the data also.

**[05:17:23.480] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003855)  
Mr. Speaker.

**[05:17:25.580] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003856)  
Maraming salamat po, Distinguished Colleague.

**[05:17:28.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003857)  
Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor, dahil nabanggit ko na ang tungkol sa mga safety deficiencies,

**[05:17:36.280] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003858)  
dadako naman ako sa MV June Astor Tragedy kung saan 77, ulitin ko po, 77 na ang naitalang namatay.

**[05:17:48.580] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003859)  
Recently, it was reported that the ship's captain voluntarily surfaced and appeared before the authorities.

**[05:17:56.260] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003860)  
Sa mga press statements din,

**[05:17:58.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003861)  
nabanggit na Department of Transportation mismo ang nag-i-investigate ng nasabing kaso.

**[05:18:05.380] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003862)  
Given this development, may formal findings na po ba ang DOTR kung ano ang rason o sanhi ng trahedya?

**[05:18:14.880] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003863)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[05:18:17.480] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003864)  
Interim Marine Casualty Investigation is available but final report is expected to be completed by end of this month.

**[05:18:26.180] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003865)  
Since wala pa pong final,

**[05:18:28.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003866)  
ano po kaya ang timeline sa investigation at overall resolution ng case?

**[05:18:35.380] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003867)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[05:18:38.220] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003868)  
ang safety certificate ng dalawang operating vessel ng Atienza Ferries were suspended.

**[05:18:47.600] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003869)  
Thus, they are not allowed to operate, Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague.

**[05:18:52.380] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003870)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[05:18:53.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003871)  
May suspension o revocation na po ba ang...

**[05:18:58.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003872)  
let's say licenses or permits na ginawa ang marina kaugnay sa trahedya kasama ang iba pa nilang mga barko kung sakaling mayroon man?

**[05:19:12.140] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003873)  
Mayroon na po, Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague.

**[05:19:16.660] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003874)  
Ang dalawang operating vessel ng Atienza Ferries ay suspended na po sa ngayon at hindi na po sila hinahayaang makapaglayag.

**[05:19:27.420] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003875)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[05:19:28.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003876)  
Good sponsor, maaari bang ipaliwanag bakit hindi grinound immediately ang fleet ng Atienza Interisland Ferries Incorporated na operator ng MV June Aster habang ongoing ang investigation?

**[05:19:42.860] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003877)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[05:19:45.720] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003878)  
ang iba pong operating na vessel ng Atienza were subjected to verification, inspection, and subsequently safety certificate were suspended.

**[05:19:55.980] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003879)  
And now...

**[05:19:58.300] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003880)  
not operating.

**[05:20:00.260] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003881)  
Number two,

**[05:20:01.600] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003882)  
marina has no existing policy on fleet suspension or walang automatic suspension ng lahat ng barko.

**[05:20:10.500] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003883)  
Suspension depends on the result of compliance verification per vessel.

**[05:20:17.060] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003884)  
And number three,

**[05:20:18.540] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003885)  
this is the first very serious incident involving Atienza ships.

**[05:20:22.480] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003886)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague.

**[05:20:25.220] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003887)  
Maraming salamat.

**[05:20:26.020] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003888)  
Sa pagklaro po dito, no?

**[05:20:28.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003889)  
Ano ba ang mga requisites bago mag-ground ang fleet ng isang shipping company or operator?

**[05:20:35.040] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003890)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[05:20:37.500] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003891)  
Number one,

**[05:20:39.720] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003892)  
fleet suspension or grounding will be implemented if the safety management system of the company is found to be uncompliant with international safety management code.

**[05:20:54.080] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003893)  
Number two, Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[05:20:56.820] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003894)  
major safety deficiencies,

**[05:20:58.300] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003895)  
on all vessels of all the particular company,

**[05:21:02.900] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003896)  
major deficiencies are identified in the marina ship safety survey manual.

**[05:21:10.200] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003897)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague.

**[05:21:11.960] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003898)  
As a follow-up po, Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor,

**[05:21:15.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003899)  
kung hindi pwedeng i-ground ang fleet ng isang company na involved sa isang shipping tragedy,

**[05:21:20.380] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003900)  
ano ang hakbang na ginagawa ng DOTR at marina to ensure na ang remaining vessels ng isang shipping company,

**[05:21:28.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003901)  
ay seaworthy?

**[05:21:30.860] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003902)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[05:21:33.160] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003903)  
magkakandak po ng safety verification inspection of the company's vessel.

**[05:21:38.500] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003904)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague.

**[05:21:40.560] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003905)  
Ano po ang immediate reforms na ipapatupad ng marina matapos ang trahedyang ito?

**[05:21:47.800] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003906)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[05:21:50.920] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003907)  
number one, reiteration of the safety protocols,

**[05:21:55.240] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003908)  
particularly,

**[05:21:55.300] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003909)  
the conduct of emergency,

**[05:21:58.300] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003910)  
emergency readiness evaluation.

**[05:22:01.820] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003911)  
Number two,

**[05:22:03.140] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003912)  
fast-track the issuance of rules on the carriage of EVs and HEVs.

**[05:22:10.500] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003913)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague.

**[05:22:13.000] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003914)  
Under the 2027 NEP for the Maritime Industry Promotion and Development Program,

**[05:22:20.160] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003915)  
target ng marina na magkaroon ng seven policies formulated, updated, issued,

**[05:22:27.900] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003916)  
and distributed.

**[05:22:28.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003917)  
Good sponsor, can marina confirm if the target has been reached?

**[05:22:36.080] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003918)  
Yes po, Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague.

**[05:22:38.620] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003919)  
Ano-ano rin po ang mga policies na pinormulate at in-update ng marina sa taong ito?

**[05:22:48.220] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003920)  
Meron po tatlo tungkol po sa SECW.

**[05:22:55.020] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003921)  
Maritime Education and Training ng mga seafarers.

**[05:23:01.320] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003923)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor,

**[05:23:04.320] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003924)  
this representation would like to request a copy of all the data related to this policy, po.

**[05:23:11.620] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003925)  
Can the DOTR and Marina commit to provide them before we move to the second reading of the GAA?

**[05:23:19.020] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003926)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[05:23:21.240] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003927)  
Marina will submit the necessary document to your office.

**[05:23:25.140] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003928)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[05:23:27.260] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003929)  
Good sponsor,

**[05:23:28.140] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003930)  
na-reflect na ba or nakapag-reflect na ba sa mga policies na ito

**[05:23:34.660] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003931)  
ang mga dapat sinupin base sa mga naging aral ng MV

**[05:23:40.520] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003932)  
Trisha Kerstin 3 Tragedy at MV June Aster Tragedy na parehas nangyari ngayong taon?

**[05:23:52.820] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003933)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[05:23:55.320] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003934)  
lahat ng ito po ay nakita.

**[05:23:58.140] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003935)  
At isa po sa counter-measure na ginawa ng ahensya ay tinaasan po ang fines and penalties

**[05:24:04.900] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003936)  
pag-ating po sa mga violation ng mga kumpanya,

**[05:24:08.400] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003937)  
nag-violate po pag-ating po sa mga nangyaring aksidente o insidente.

**[05:24:12.660] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003938)  
Nagsasagawa ba ang Marina ng stakeholders consultation bago maglabas ng bagong policies?

**[05:24:19.020] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003939)  
O ito ay para matiyak na ang reforms ay grounded sa aktwal na karanasan ng industriya at nangyari?

**[05:24:28.140] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003940)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague, nagsasagawa po sila lagi.

**[05:24:36.540] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003941)  
Finally, Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor,

**[05:24:39.900] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003942)  
given repeated tragedies and the billions allocated for regulation and supervision,

**[05:24:45.980] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003943)  
how can Marina assure the Filipino people that its mandate will translate into real safety outcomes,

**[05:24:53.060] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003944)  
not just paper compliance and post-incident reforms?

**[05:24:58.140] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003945)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[05:25:01.200] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003946)  
ang Marina ay nagbibigay ng mahigpit na patakaran at nagbibigay ng mahigpit na at seryosong penalties

**[05:25:12.680] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003947)  
sa lahat na magba-violate ng safety ng mga mananakay natin.

**[05:25:19.980] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003948)  
At nagpo-focus sila sa mga drills at ang actual na implementation ay pinatutupad nila

**[05:25:26.420] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003949)

Mr. Speaker,

**[05:35:12.140] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004050)

**[05:35:19.320] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004051)  
dahil nakita na po sa pagpaplano, sa pagdedesign,

**[05:35:23.500] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004052)

**[05:35:33.480] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004053)

**[05:35:36.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004054)

**[05:35:53.500] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004057)  
Ito po ay mismo umuupo doon sa mga pinapatawag na pagpupulong ng napakahalaga na committee na ito.

**[05:36:01.480] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-004058)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[05:36:10.960] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-004060)

**[05:36:15.860] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-004061)

**[05:36:26.620] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004063)

**[05:36:32.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004064)

**[05:36:37.620] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004065)

**[05:36:42.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004066)

**[05:36:49.240] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004067)

**[05:36:50.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004070)  
Ang DOTR ang aasahan na dapat magsalita dyan.

**[05:36:54.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004071)

**[05:37:04.680] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004073)

**[05:37:10.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004074)

**[05:37:14.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004075)

**[05:37:19.500] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004076)

**[05:37:25.260] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004077)

**[05:37:33.420] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004078)

**[05:37:41.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004079)

**[05:37:48.320] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004080)

**[05:37:54.980] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004081)

**[05:38:03.640] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004082)  
ang ating pansamantalang babawasan, dapat nga pag-aralan din ng DOF ang pagtatanggal ng 12 percent na value-added tax.

**[05:38:11.560] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004083)

**[05:38:18.320] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004084)

**[05:38:25.300] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004085)  
kasama ng maraming nananawagan ngayon, dapat agaran na aksyonan ang pagpapababa ng presyo ng langis.

**[05:38:34.180] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-004086)

**[05:38:45.080] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-004087)

**[05:38:48.320] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-004088)

**[05:39:03.260] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004089)

**[05:39:18.320] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004091)

**[05:39:20.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004092)

**[05:39:34.640] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004093)  
na huwag naman po sanang maipasan lahat sa taong bayan na lang yung bigat po ng taas ng presyo ng langis.

**[05:39:42.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004094)  
Sa bahagi din po ng pamahalaan, kailangan sa atin din po.

**[05:39:48.320] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004095)  
Bahagi ng pagsasakripisyo.

**[05:39:50.340] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004096)

**[05:39:55.580] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004097)

**[05:39:58.300] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004098)
